### [61] [Chameleon: Recovering Cyber-Physical Systems from Memory Corruption Attacks via ML Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01356)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chameleon是一个利用机器学习代理自动恢复受内存损坏攻击的CPS的框架，在攻击检测后用无漏洞的代理替换受损模块，通过LLVM实现并在7种机器人车辆上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS安全技术要么在检测到攻击后终止/暂停执行（对安全关键任务不可接受），要么用简化默认组件降级系统行为，缺乏在攻击发生后自动恢复系统正常功能的方法。

Method: 基于LLVM编译器实现，以隔离区（compartment）为粒度训练ML代理，使其近乎复制原始隔离区行为但不包含相同内存损坏漏洞；攻击检测后替换受损隔离区为其训练好的代理。

Result: 在7种不同机器人车辆（含模拟和真实）上评估，代理平均R²=0.96，能成功从真实内存攻击中恢复并完成任务，性能与内存开销低。

Conclusion: Chameleon提供了一种可行的、低开销的自动化CPS攻击恢复方案，优于仅检测/终止或降级行为的现有方法。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly deployed in every aspect of our lives and can be compromised through memory corruption vulnerabilities, allowing attackers to hijack the control flow and take over the system. Existing techniques mostly focus on detecting such attacks but respond by terminating or halting execution upon attack detection, which is not acceptable in CPSs used in safety-critical tasks, as interrupted tasks can have catastrophic consequences. Other techniques replace compromised CPS components with simplified defaults that degrade system behavior, or reboot the system upon attack detection.
  We propose Chameleon, a novel framework for automatically recovering CPSs from memory corruption attacks using machine learning (ML)-based surrogates trained at compartment granularity that nearly replicate their original compartments' behavior but do not have the same memory corruption vulnerabilities. Upon attack detection, Chameleon replaces the compromised compartment with its trained surrogate. We implemented Chameleon using the LLVM compiler and evaluated its efficiency and effectiveness on seven different robotic vehicles (RVs), including simulated and real ones. We found that Chameleon can generate surrogates that closely approximate the original compartments (with an average R$^2$=0.96), successfully recover the system despite real-world memory corruption attacks unlike prior approaches, and complete their tasks while incurring low performance and memory overhead.

</details>


### [62] [Cognitive Firewall: A Proactive, Zero-Trust, Multi-Gate Framework for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01277)
*Michele Guida,Ruslan Shikhhamzayev,Sindhuja Penchala,Stefano Iannucci,Jiacheng Li,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出认知防火墙（Cognitive Firewall），一种通过四个分类门（意图门、零信任上下文门、一致性门、输出风险门）对多轮对话进行逐层分解评估的主动运行时安全框架，在多个越狱基准测试上将攻击成功率降至2%以下，同时保持仅8%的过度拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全机制通常将提示或响应作为孤立的单一消息进行评估，无法恢复多轮对话中累积的恶意意图、验证声称的权限，或检测跨多轮分解的有害目标。

Method: 提出认知防火墙框架，在用户与目标模型之间插入独立的监督模型，将安全评估分解为四个分类门：意图门（识别请求的操作目标）、零信任上下文门（将声称的角色和权限视为未验证证据）、一致性门（检测跨轮次的升级与分解）、输出风险门（在释放前检查候选响应）。通过升级机制（而非平均打分）组合各门决策，任一门的危险信号即可阻止交互。

Result: 在四个越狱基准测试和一个良性安全测试集上，认知防火墙将攻击成功率降至2%或以下（三个攻击集），在最难的人工构造攻击集上降至14%，同时保持仅8%的过度拒绝率。

Conclusion: 分解式的、对话级别的监督能够改善LLM安全的主动防御能力和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be induced to produce harmful content through multi turn strategies in which no single user message appears clearly unsafe. Existing runtime safeguards commonly evaluate prompts or responses as isolated messages, which limits their ability to recover ac-cumulated intent, verify asserted authority, or detect harmful objectives decomposed across a dialogue. This paper presents the Cognitive Firewall, a proactive runtime oversight framework that interposes an independent oversight model between a user and a protected target mod l. The framework decomposes safety assessment into four categorical gates: an intent gate that identi-fies the operational objective of a request, a zero trust context gate that treats claimed roles and permissions as unverified evidence, a consistency gate that detects escalation and decomposition across turns, and an output risk gate that inspects candidate responses before release. Gate decisions are combined through escalation rather than score averaging, allowing any confident danger signal to block an interaction while preserving an auditable rationale. Experiments on four jailbreak benchmarks and a benign safety test set show that the Cognitive Firewall substantially reduces attack success across single turn, multi turn, authority based, and human crafted attacks. It lowers attack success to 2 percent or below on three attack sets and to 14 percent on the most difficult human crafted set, while maintaining an 8 percent over refusal rate. These results indicate that decomposed, conversation level oversight can improve proactive containment and auditability for LLM safety.

</details>


### [63] [Generative AI and Federated Learning for Intrusion Detection Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01305)
*Jiefei Liu,Abu Saleh Md Tayeen,Pratyay Kumar,Qixu Gong,Wenbin Jiang,Huiping Cao,Satyajayant Misra,Jayashree Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了生成式AI与联邦学习在入侵检测系统中的联合应用，系统梳理了自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM等生成模型在异常检测、数据增强、流量生成等方面的作用，以及FL在隐私保护分布式IDS训练中的应用，并讨论了数据质量、非IID分布、通信效率、对抗风险等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IDS面临攻击行为演变、真实数据集难以获取、数据不完整、类别不均衡以及隐私限制等挑战，而生成式AI和联邦学习的新进展为克服这些局限提供了机遇。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，首先总结IDS研究方向（对抗机器学习、异常检测、物联网IDS、可解释IDS、基准数据集）；然后按模型族和任务目标分类生成式AI应用（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）；最后综述生成式AI与FL相结合的IDS研究。

Result: 系统分类了生成式AI在IDS中的多种应用场景（异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强、数据插补、对抗流量生成、告警解释），以及FL支持分布式隐私保护IDS训练的优势。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战：合成数据质量、真实流量生成、双重用途对抗风险、非IID客户端分布、通信高效的模型共享、联邦IDS基准测试、面向网络安全的领域专用LLM。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for monitoring network traffic and identifying malicious activities in modern cyber-physical, Internet of Things (IoT), enterprise, and distributed network environments. However, developing reliable IDS models remains challenging because attack behaviors evolve over time, realistic datasets are difficult to obtain, traffic records may be incomplete, attack classes are often imbalanced, and privacy constraints limit centralized data collection. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and Federated Learning (FL) provide new opportunities to address these limitations. Generative models can support anomaly detection, synthetic traffic generation, data augmentation, data imputation, adversarial traffic generation, and IDS alert explanation. FL enables distributed IDS training without directly sharing local network traffic, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive and geographically distributed environments. This survey provides a structured review of generative AI and FL techniques for IDS. We first summarize representative IDS research directions, including adversarial machine learning, anomaly-based detection, IoT-oriented IDS, explainable IDS, and benchmark datasets. We then categorize generative AI applications in IDS according to model families and task objectives, covering autoencoder-based models, Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), diffusion models, and Large Language Models (LLMs). Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

</details>


### [64] [An alternative approach towards attacks against fully-split PLWE instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01340)
*Iván Blanco-Chacón,Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma,Raúl Durán Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于根的密码攻击的泛化问题，通过构造显式同构证明了在完全分裂多项式环设置下，这类攻击无法产生新的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答关于基于根的攻击能否通过显式同构扩展到更广泛设置的关键问题。

Method: 首先构造从完全分裂多项式环到先前攻击适用多项式环的显式同构，证明该同构会扭曲样本使其无法用于区分；然后证明完全分裂多项式环间的任何同构都必须具有该构造形式。

Result: 在完全分裂设置下，通过同构方法无法产生新的漏洞。

Conclusion: 基于根的攻击在完全分裂多项式环设置下无法通过同构构造进行有效泛化。

Abstract: In the present work we address some key questions regarding the generalization of root-based attacks presented in a recent work by the authors. In particular, we analyze potential root-based attacks extensions via the construction of explicit isomorphisms from vulnerable instances, and provide a formal proof that this approach will not yield any new vulnerabilities under a fully-split setting. To do so, we first construct an explicit isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings and polynomial rings where previous attacks apply and show that the application of such an isomorphism will always distort the samples in a way that the resulting samples cannot be used to distinguish. Then, we prove that any isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings must be of the form of the constructed isomorphism.

</details>


### [65] [From Forgeries to Foundation Models: A Systematic Survey of Identity Document Attack and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01442)
*Gourab Das,Pavan Kumar C,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次统一了身份文档伪造的威胁模型，涵盖物理呈现攻击、数字注入攻击和生成式AI合成，系统审计了2019-2025年公开数据集的现实鸿沟，发现最强模型在安全操作条件下APCER仍超25%，并识别出非拉丁文字修复中的脚本依赖生成不稳定（SDGI）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具使得高保真文档伪造和字段级篡改变得极易实现，而现有检测方法受限于无法反映这一威胁的基准测试，导致物理呈现、数字注入和完全生成式合成三种攻击面缺乏统一的威胁模型和评估框架。

Method: 采用统一身份验证威胁模型，系统梳理从基于规则的启发式方法到取证定位、注入感知流水线、基础模型和少样本框架的检测方法学演进；对2019-2025年公共数据集进行系统审计以揭示现实鸿沟；分析大型多模态模型在身份文档篡改中的表现，识别SDGI故障模式；在未见过的合成ID卡上进行零样本基准测试。

Result: 识别出脚本依赖生成不稳定（SDGI）作为非拉丁文字修复中的重复性排版故障模式；最强公开模型在安全导向操作条件下APCER值仍超过25%，表明跨域泛化能力存在显著局限性。

Conclusion: 未来方向应朝向取证基础扎实、隐私保护和法律可问责的身份验证系统发展。

Abstract: Identity document forgery has undergone a fundamental capability shift: generative AI tools now enable high-fidelity document synthesis and field-level manipulation with minimal technical expertise, while detection methods remain constrained by benchmarks that do not reflect this threat. The resulting attack surface spans physical presentation, digital injection, and fully generative synthesis, introducing distinct forensic failure modes that require a unified threat model and evaluation framework. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified treatment of Presentation Attacks, Digital Injection Attacks, and GenAI-driven synthesis within a single identity verification threat model. We trace detection methodologies from rule-based heuristics through forensic localisation, injection-aware pipelines, foundation models, and few-shot frameworks. A systematic audit of public datasets from 2019--2025 exposes a persistent Reality Gap between benchmark conditions and operational deployment. We further analyse large multimodal models for identity document manipulation, identifying Script-Dependent Generative Instability (SDGI) as a recurring typographic failure mode in non-Latin script inpainting. Finally, zero-shot benchmarking on unseen synthesised ID cards shows that even the strongest publicly available models achieve APCER values above 25% under security-oriented operating conditions, highlighting substantial limits in cross-domain generalisation. We conclude by outlining future directions toward forensically grounded, privacy-preserving, and legally accountable identity verification systems.

</details>


### [66] [Hamm-Grams: An Algorithm for Mining Regular Expressions of Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01445)
*Derek Everett,Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为hamm-grams的新型鲁棒特征（固定长度带单字符通配符的正则表达式），用于恶意软件分类与检测，通过设计 locality-sensitive hash 和聚类算法高效生成这些特征，克服了传统 n-gram 特征脆弱性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的n-gram特征是恶意软件机器学习检测系统中常用的静态特征，但这类特征本质上是脆弱的（brittle），容易被对抗性修改绕过。因此需要构建更鲁棒的特征来提升恶意软件检测系统的健壮性。

Method: 提出hamm-grams特征，即一类固定长度且包含单字符通配符的特殊正则表达式。设计了一种高效的算法来发现常见的hamm-grams，包括：1）一种新的 locality-sensitive hash（局部敏感哈希），用于在小汉明距离的样本对之间产生碰撞；2）在哈希桶内进行聚类以确定通配符的位置。

Result: 在恶意软件分类与检测任务上，实验证明了hamm-grams特征相比传统n-gram特征的优越性。

Conclusion: hamm-grams作为一种新型鲁棒静态特征，能够有效提升恶意软件检测系统的性能，为对抗恶意软件变种和对抗性攻击提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Malware poses a critical and ever-evolving threat, and robust and effective systems for detecting and classifying malware are of essential importance. $n$-grams features are among the common static features used in effective machine learning systems for malware, but these features are inherently brittle. We propose an algorithm for constructing more robust features, hamm-grams, which are a special class of regular expressions having a fixed length and single-character wildcards. We devise an efficient algorithm for finding common hamm-grams using a new locality-sensitive hash designed to produce collisions among pairs of small Hamming distance and a clustering within hash buckets to place wildcards. We then demonstrate the advantages of these features in malware classification and detection tasks.

</details>


### [67] [Overthink-Triggered Slowdown Attacks on LVLM-Based Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01518)
*Qiang Han,Jie Wu,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了大视觉语言模型（LVLM）在机器人系统中的"过度思考"行为，即生成过长推理链导致推理延迟。作者提出了一种三阶段框架，通过嵌入精心构造的、人类可读的场景文本来触发过度思考，从而实现延迟放大攻击。实验表明，文本触发向量在不同LVLM模型间具有可迁移性，最强单触发情况下延迟放大比达6.96倍，物理打印文本触发仍可导致高达4.74倍的延迟放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在机器人系统中广泛应用，但其过度思考行为会导致推理时间过长。攻击者可以利用这一行为，通过嵌入特定场景文本故意触发过度思考，从而拖慢机器人决策速度，引发安全问题。现有研究尚未系统性地识别和验证这种攻击的可迁移触发向量。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(1) 构建推理密集型场景文本语料库，从短响应前缀中提取与过度思考相关的词汇特征；(2) 基于前缀代理分数进行高效的黑盒搜索，对少量顶级候选者进行完整延迟测量确认；(3) 在固定触发池上评估黑盒迁移性，报告延迟放大比和攻击成功率。

Result: 在三个代表性LVLM上，所有触发向量均产生大于1.0x的减速比，最强单触发达6.96x。物理打印文本触发仍可导致4.74x的延迟放大。

Conclusion: 发现的可迁移触发向量能够在多个LVLM模型之间传递，并一致性地对机器人系统造成显著的减速效果，揭示了LVLM在安全关键型机器人应用中的一个重要漏洞。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have been increasingly integrated into robotic systems. However, these models may exhibit overthinking behaviors, where they generate excessively long reasoning traces, incurring an excessive inference time. This overthinking behavior poses a serious risk to robotic systems, as the adversary can deliberately trigger overthinking to slow down the decision making of a victim robotic system, causing a variety of safety issues (i.e., an overthinking-induced slowdown attack). To initiate this attack, an adversary can embed carefully crafted, human-readable scene text into the visual scene observed by a victim robotic agent, causing significant inference delays even under a strict black-box setting. Therefore, the embedded scene text serves as a significant "trigger" for the attack.
  This work systematically identifies and validates transferable triggers of overthinking in robotic systems by introducing a three-stage framework. First, we construct a diverse corpus of reasoning-intensive scene text and extract overthinking-correlated lexical features from short response prefixes. Second, we perform an efficient black-box search guided by a prefix-based proxy score while selectively confirming a small set of top candidates with full latency measurements. Third, we evaluate black-box transfer using a fixed pool of triggers on unseen images and multiple LVLMs, reporting latency amplification and attack success rates under standard thresholds. Across three representative LVLMs, all triggers yield slowdown ratios greater than 1.0x, with the strongest single-trigger case reaching 6.96x. The physical printing of the text trigger still causes up to 4.74x latency amplification. These results demonstrate that our discovered triggers are transferred between multiple LVLM models and consistently cause significant slowdowns in robotic systems.

</details>


### [68] [LIB-TRAP: Standard Cell Library Hardware Trojan Risk Assessment and Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01526)
*Harish Kumar Dharavath,Md Muhtasim Alam Chowdhury,Rozhin Yasaei,Soheil Salehi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型硬件木马威胁模型，将标准单元视为不可信来源。通过将标准单元库转换为恶意库，攻击者可在不改变网表结构的情况下插入任意硬件木马。在Saed32nm和Sky130nm工艺上，利用AES-128、以太网控制器和DMA引擎等基准电路验证了该攻击的有效性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件木马研究多聚焦于标准单元间的空间，而缺乏对构成绝大多数数字设计基础的标准单元本身脆弱性的研究。本文旨在探索标准单元被恶意篡改的新型威胁模型。

Method: 将现有标准单元库（Saed32nm和Sky130nm）转换为恶意库，其中包含失活的HT单元和激活的HT单元。设计方使用含失活HT的库进行综合，代工厂在制造时替换为激活HT单元。使用开源和工业标准EDA工具，在多个基准设计（AES-128、以太网控制器、WISHBONE DMA引擎）上进行综合和表征。

Result: 在Synopsys 32nm和SkyWater 130nm工艺上，三个基准电路（AES-128、以太网控制器、DMA引擎）使用干净库和木马感染库分别综合。提取了总单元数、总面积、动态功耗和静态功耗等设计级特征，作为二元分类的输入。

Conclusion: 基于标准细胞的硬件木马攻击向量具有有效性和隐蔽性，能够从IC设计者面前隐藏任意HT的存在，对硬件安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities inherent to the fabless semiconductor manufacturing model have significantly increased the risk of malicious Hardware Trojan (HT) insertion, posing severe threats to hardware security. Several HT mitigation and detection strategies have been developed, and existing works explore the insertion of HTs in the space between standard cells in an integrated circuit. However, there is a lack of research into the vulnerabilities posed by the building blocks of most digital designs on the market today, the standard cells. This work investigates a novel threat model in which standard cells are considered untrusted. Our proposed threat model provides the design house with a tampered standard cell library. The intended netlist is synthesized and implemented using the tampered library. During fabrication, a nefarious foundry replaces the library's deactivated HT cells with activated counterparts. Using open-source and industry-standard Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tools, existing standard cell libraries, Saed32nm and Sky130nm, are converted into malicious libraries capable of masking the presence of arbitrary HTs from IC designers. The malicious library is then applied and characterized in multiple standard benchmark designs. To demonstrate the efficacy and stealthiness of this standard cell-based attack vector, three benchmark circuits, an AES-128 encryption core, an Ethernet controller, and a WISHBONE DMA engine, were synthesized using both clean and Trojan-infected libraries across Synopsys 32nm and SkyWater 130nm technologies. Design-level features, including total cell count, total area, dynamic power consumption, and static power, were extracted from these synthesized circuits to serve as inputs for binary classification

</details>


### [69] [VeriChat: An Agentic Conversational AI Assistant for Hardware Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01668)
*Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Shams Tarek,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriChat 是一个面向硬件安全验证的领域专用对话助手，采用检索增强的多智能体工作流来减少幻觉，并集成开源 EDA 工具（Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys）以支持语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证。在 AES S-Box 硬件木马检测案例中，VeriChat 通过多轮对话自主识别、仿真并形式化证明了隐蔽的密钥泄露漏洞，Faithfulness 得分达 87.73%，显著优于主流闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全验证流程复杂，工程师缺乏结构化的安全指导；现有的通用聊天机器人（如 ChatGPT、Gemini）容易产生幻觉且依赖静态知识，无法安全可靠地提供硬件安全领域的实时上下文感知帮助。

Method: 提出 VeriChat，采用检索增强生成（RAG）结合多智能体工作流：三个专业化智能体协同工作以最小化幻觉并提升响应透明度与可靠性；同时集成 Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys 等开源 EDA 工具，对用户提供的 RTL 设计直接执行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证。

Result: VeriChat 的 Faithfulness 得分为 87.73%，显著优于主流闭源大模型。在 AES S-Box 硬件木马检测案例中，VeriChat 通过多轮对话自主完成了隐蔽密钥泄露漏洞的识别、仿真和形式化证明。

Conclusion: VeriChat 作为领域专用的对话助手，能够有效支持而非取代现有硬件安全验证流程，在提升安全咨询可靠性方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Hardware security verification is a multi-stage process in which engineers must navigate complex design analyses, threat considerations, and verification strategies. They often need security-focused guidance, yet current verification environments provide little structured support for such assistance. Although conversational AI could offer such on-demand assistance, directly using general-purpose chatbots like ChatGPT or Gemini is risky due to their tendency to hallucinate and their reliance on static, outdated knowledge. We present VeriChat, a domain-specialized conversational assistant designed to support, rather than replace, existing verification workflows by providing context-aware security guidance. VeriChat employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent workflow in which three specialized agents collaboratively minimize hallucinations while improving the transparency and reliability of the response. Beyond question answering, VeriChat integrates open-source EDA tools, including Icarus Verilog, Yosys, and SymbiYosys, to perform syntax checking, synthesis analysis, simulation, and formal verification directly on user-provided RTL designs. Evaluated using a comprehensive methodology, VeriChat achieves a Faithfulness score of 87.73%, significantly outperforming the leading proprietary models. We demonstrate the framework through a hardware Trojan detection case study on an AES S-Box IP, where VeriChat autonomously identifies, simulates, and formally proves a covert key-leakage vulnerability through a multi-turn conversational workflow.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Gradient-Based Attacks: Adversarial Robustness and Explainability Stability in Cybersecurity Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01679)
*Mona Rajhans,Vishal Khawarey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗攻击对网络安全分类器及其SHAP解释稳定性的影响，提出可解释性稳定性指数（ESI），发现预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是两个独立维度，需联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗攻击不仅降低分类器预测准确率，还会破坏安全分析师依赖的SHAP解释稳定性，现有工作缺乏对树模型解释稳定性的系统评估和量化指标。

Method: 将先前的MLP研究扩展到Random Forest和XGBoost，使用四个表格安全数据集（phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021），评估五种攻击方法（含三种黑盒方法）；提出ESI指标衡量TreeSHAP在对抗扰动下的漂移；采用双轴框架（梯度依赖、查询效率）解释攻击排名。

Result: 基于梯度的ZOO攻击对XGBoost产生退化结果（RI~0.98），而基于得分的Square Attack揭示真实脆弱性（RI~0.36）；退化扰动仍导致XGBoost ESI为0.06-0.16，RF为0.14-0.29，表明预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是独立维度；步长消融实验解释了z-score归一化表格数据上的PGD异常现象。

Conclusion: 预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是正交的评估维度，需联合测量；梯度依赖性和查询效率构成的双轴框架可为树集成模型评估提供实用指导。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on cybersecurity classifiers pose a dual threat: degrading predictions and destabilising the SHAP-based explanations that security analysts rely on to understand and triage alerts. We extend our prior MLP conference study to Random Forest and XGBoost across four tabular security datasets (phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021), evaluating five attacks including three black-box methods applicable to non-differentiable tree models. We introduce the Explainability Stability Index (ESI), a scalar metric computed from TreeSHAP attribution drift under adversarial perturbation, reported on the same [0,1] scale as the Robustness Index (RI). A key finding is that gradient-based black-box attacks (ZOO) produce degenerate results against XGBoost (apparent RI ~0.98) due to piecewise-constant prediction surfaces, while score-based Square Attack reveals genuine vulnerability (RI ~0.36). These degenerate perturbations still drive substantial attribution drift: XGBoost ESI ~0.06-0.16 despite near-perfect ZOO robustness, versus 0.14-0.29 for RF, showing that prediction robustness and explanation stability are distinct axes requiring joint measurement. A two-axis framework (gradient dependence, query efficiency) explains the observed attack ranking and yields practical guidance for tree ensemble evaluation. A step-size ablation explains a counterintuitive PGD anomaly on z-score normalised tabular data.

</details>


### [71] [Pmeta-TLA: Backdoor Attacks for Speech Classification Models via Meta-Learning with Timbre Leakage Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01702)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于音色泄露攻击（TLA）的后门攻击方法，通过在深度自监督特征的帧级别嵌入音色信息生成自然样本，并引入Pmeta-TLA利用元学习和PCGrad实现多后门一次性注入，在关键词识别任务中实现了高攻击效能、强隐蔽性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后门攻击对语音分类模型构成严重安全威胁，但现有语音触发器容易被深度神经网络防御器检测，亟需探索新的攻击技术以揭露并防范此类风险。

Method: 提出TLA（Timbre Leakage Attack），在深度自监督特征的帧级别嵌入音色信息，生成对人类感知自然的毒化样本；提出Pmeta-TLA，利用元学习和Projected Conflicting Gradients（PCGrad）实现多后门一次性注入训练，并将TLA作为多目标攻击工具集成其中。

Result: 在关键词识别任务中使用多种深度神经网络模型进行数据投毒后门攻击实验，结果表明所提方法在攻击效能、隐蔽性、鲁棒性方面优于基线方法，且攻击成本更低。

Conclusion: TLA和Pmeta-TLA提供了更高效、更隐蔽、更鲁棒的后门攻击方案，有助于揭示语音分类模型的安全漏洞并推动防御研究。

Abstract: Recently, speech classification methods have gained widespread adoption in intelligent gadgets. Current study indicates that backdoor attacks provide a substantial security concern to these models, underscoring the pressing necessity to investigate additional potential attack techniques to expose and prevent such risks. This work discusses the vulnerability of current speech triggers to detection by deep neural network defenders and introduces the Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA). The suggested trigger disseminates timbre information at the frame level within the deep self-supervised features, producing poisoned samples that appear natural to human perception. Furthermore, we introduce Pmeta-TLA, an innovative training mechanism for embedding numerous backdoors one time. This method proposes a multi-backdoor injection training strategy using meta-learning and Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad) and introduces TLA as a multi-target attack tool within it. We performed tests on data-poisoning backdoor attacks in keyword spotting tasks utilizing some deep neural network models. Experimental results indicate that the proposed strategy attains superior Attack efficacy, enhanced stealthiness, robustness, and a reduced attack cost relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [72] [Trust Boundary Semantic Gaps: A Multi-dimensional Analysis and Mitigation for Security-by-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01711)
*Doyeon Kim,Jin-Young Choi,Junghee Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出信任边界语义鸿沟（TBSG）概念，指构件穿越信任边界时通过正确的句法验证，但所建立的断言不足以满足接收域安全需求。基于75起安全事件（2014-2025）分析，构建四维分析模型（MDTBSG）及设计阶段框架TBSAM，并在SolarWinds/SUNBURST攻击中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有格式、协议和签名等句法验证机制仅能确保构件格式正确、协议合规或认证有效，无法保证构件满足接收域的语义安全属性。因此，通过句法验证的有符号更新或认证令牌仍可能导致系统被攻破，亟需揭示并弥合这一语义层面的安全鸿沟。

Method: 1) 分析2014-2025年间75起公开安全事件，在信任边界层面进行归因；2) 提出四维语义对齐分析模型MDTBSG：身份（Identity）、空间（Spatial）、时间（Temporal）、解释（Interpretation）；3) 构建设计阶段框架TBSAM，从设计规范中识别TBSG、优先级排序、追溯传播的鸿沟至其起源边界，并映射到候选架构控制措施；4) 对SolarWinds/SUNBURST供应链攻击进行回顾性重建验证。

Result: TBSAM能够显式化接收域假设、区分本地起源与传播而来的鸿沟，并识别出能够中断攻击路径的控制措施。验证表明句法验证在信任边界处是不充分的，显式化信任边界假设可补充Security-by-Design方法。

Conclusion: 句法验证在信任边界处是必要但不充分的，将信任边界假设显式化是Security-by-Design的有效补充；TBSG框架为设计阶段识别和缓解语义安全鸿沟提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Modern systems use format-, protocol-, and signature-based mechanisms before accepting artifacts across trust boundaries. These mechanisms are necessary: they show that an artifact is well formed, protocol-compliant, or properly authenticated. They do not, however, show that the artifact satisfies the semantic security properties required by the receiving domain. A signed update or an authenticated token may therefore be accepted yet enable compromise. We call this condition a Trust Boundary Semantic Gap (TBSG): an artifact crosses a trust boundary and passes correctly implemented syntactic validation, but the assertions established by that pass are insufficient to satisfy the receiving domain's security requirements. TBSG concerns what remains unestablished after a syntactic pass, not absent checks or implementation bugs. Analyzing 75 publicly reported security incidents (2014-2025) at the boundary level, we organize semantic misalignment into a four-dimensional analysis model: Identity, Spatial, Temporal, and Interpretation (MDTBSG). Building on it, we develop Trust Boundary Semantic Analysis and Mitigation (TBSAM), a design-time framework that identifies TBSGs from design specifications, prioritizes them, traces propagated gaps to their originating boundary, and maps each to candidate architectural controls. We apply TBSAM to a retrospective reconstruction of the SolarWinds/SUNBURST supply-chain attack, showing how it makes receiving-domain assumptions explicit, separates locally originating from propagated gaps, and identifies controls that interrupt the path. These results suggest that syntactic validation, while necessary, is not sufficient at trust boundaries, and that making trust-boundary assumptions explicit can complement Security-by-Design.

</details>


### [73] [Resilient Liquid Democracy: Mitigating Voting Power Imbalances via Secure Delegation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01730)
*Zhuolun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于密封委托的液态民主机制，使用去中心化定时释放加密隐藏委托选择以防止羊群效应和胁迫，同时支持排名多委托和个人后备选票以应对委托人失职，并在多个真实数据集上验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液态民主部署通常采用透明委托，导致选民受人气驱动的羊群效应影响、胁迫可被验证，且当高支持率委托人弃权时系统脆弱性增加。本文旨在解决知情专业路由与系统稳健性之间的张力。

Method: 引入密封委托机制，利用去中心化定时释放加密在形成阶段隐藏委托选择，同时保持最终计票的公开可审计性；扩展协议支持排名多委托和个人后备选票以应对委托人失职；形式化证明预揭示保密性和重新提交收据免强制性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集（包含市政参与式预算选举及校准调查、另外20个参与式预算选举、6万名美国选民）上的评估表明：委托是否改进代表准确性遵循可恢复缺口定律；密封委托的好处主要是结构性的，显著降低权力集中而非直接提升准确性；排名多委托和个人后备选票在现实和针对性委托失败下大幅减少选票损失。

Conclusion: 密封委托机制有效解决了透明委托的核心缺陷，其结构性优势在于降低权力集中；委托的价值依赖于特定的条件（大量且系统性无代表性的弃权），而排名多委托与后备选票是提升系统稳健性的关键设计。

Abstract: Liquid democracy promises to improve collective decision-making by allowing voters to vote directly, delegate their voting power to trusted participants, or combine both approaches through fallback mechanisms. However, existing deployments typically rely on transparent delegation, which exposes voters to popularity-driven herding, makes coercion verifiable, and introduces systemic fragility when highly-backed delegates abstain. In this paper, we propose a secure liquid democracy mechanism that resolves the tension between informed expertise routing and systemic robustness. We introduce a sealed delegation regime using decentralized timed-release encryption, which cryptographically hides delegation choices during the formation phase to prevent herding and coercion, while restoring full public auditability for the final tally. To address delegate failures, we extend the protocol with ranked multi-delegation and personal fallback ballots. We formally prove pre-reveal secrecy and resubmission receipt-freeness for our protocol. Finally, we evaluate the mechanism on four real datasets, a municipal participatory-budgeting election with a calibration survey, twenty further participatory-budgeting elections, and 60,000 US voters with an objective competence measure. They show that whether delegation improves representational accuracy follows a recoverable-gap law; it helps only when abstention is large and systematically unrepresentative, and is otherwise neutral or harmful, with representative-style delegation safer than delegating to a competence elite. The benefit of sealed formation is primarily structural, sharply reducing power concentration rather than directly improving accuracy; and ranked multi-delegation with personal fallback ballots sharply reduces vote loss under realistic and targeted delegate failures, a result that replicates across all twenty elections.

</details>


### [74] [Knowledge Over Parameters: Evolving Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01742)
*Yuqiang Sun,Han Liu,Ying Li,Yiran Zhang,Zong Cao,Ziyun Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoVuln是一个自动化框架，将智能合约漏洞检测重构为程序性知识进化问题，仅需极少标注样本即可合成和优化检测逻辑，在5种真实漏洞类型上取得71%宏平均F1分数，且进化后的知识可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的漏洞检测方法存在局限：基于提示的方法依赖人工构建检测规则，而微调方法需要大量标注数据集，但在智能合约漏洞领域标注数据本就稀缺。因此需要一种能自动合成和优化检测逻辑、仅需少量标注样本的方法。

Method: 提出EvoVuln框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 基于控制反转架构的运行时，将检测规则编译为可执行策略，解耦确定性控制流与LLM语义推理，生成密集诊断遥测信息用于精准错误定位；(2) 两阶段进化流水线：冷启动阶段利用自动合成的边界案例引导初始规则并进行压力测试；少样本进化阶段仅用每种漏洞类型5个漏洞样本和5个安全样本，通过溯因语义调试精化规则。

Result: 在5种真实世界漏洞类型上评估，EvoVuln达到71%宏平均F1分数，优于所有基线方法。进化后的程序性知识可跨模型迁移：使轻量低成本模型超越更大的零样本模型19个百分点，且无需重新训练即可迁移到其他LLM，一次性进化成本低于50美元。

Conclusion: EvoVuln证明了将漏洞检测重构为程序性知识进化问题的有效性，仅需极少标注样本即可自动合成高质量检测逻辑，且进化后的知识具有模型可移植性，为资源受限场景下的智能合约漏洞检测提供了低成本、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities are predominantly logic bugs whose detection requires structured, step-by-step procedural knowledge of attack patterns and contract semantics. Existing LLM-based methods struggle to generate this knowledge automatically: prompt-based methods rely on manually crafted detection rules, while fine-tuning requires massive labeled datasets that are inherently scarce in this domain. We present EvoVuln, an automated framework that reformulates vulnerability detection as a procedural knowledge evolution problem, synthesizing and refining detection logic using only a minimal number of labeled samples.
  To achieve this, EvoVuln introduces two key mechanisms. First, a Runtime with an Inversion of Control (IoC) architecture compiles detection rules into Executable Policies. This strictly decouples deterministic control flow from LLM semantic reasoning, ensuring faithful logical adherence and producing dense diagnostic telemetry for precise error localization. Second, a two-phase evolution pipeline refines the rule via abductive semantic debugging without any parameter updates: Cold Start bootstraps and stress-tests an initial rule using auto-synthesized corner cases; Few-Shot Evolving then grounds the policy in real-world semantics using only five vulnerable and five safe examples per vulnerability type.
  Evaluated across five real-world vulnerability types, EvoVuln achieves a 71% macro-average F1-score, outperforming all baselines. The evolved procedural knowledge is portable across models: it enables a lightweight, low-cost model to surpass a much larger zero-shot model by 19 percentage points, and transfers to other LLMs without retraining, at a one-time evolution cost under $50.

</details>


### [75] [Has This Checkpoint Been Abliterated? A Two-Signal Audit and Its Failure Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01854)
*Gabriel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参考锚定的激活拒绝间隙和权重恢复能量的双信号检查点审计方法，无需阈值校准即可检测开放权重模型是否被去除拒绝机制。在273个检查点数据集上，z-sum指标达到AUROC 0.95，显著优于单一信号，但该方法存在两种失败模式（欺骗参考和白盒攻击），适用于有效分诊而非防篡改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署前无法判断开放权重检查点是否被剥离了拒绝机制，现有的运行时防护措施仅能评分生成内容而非检测模型工件本身。

Method: 结合两种廉价的内部信号：参考锚定的激活拒绝间隙（提供拒绝特异性）和基础到候选权重差异的权重恢复能量（提供召回率），组成无阈值检查点审计方法。

Result: 在覆盖Qwen、DeepSeek-distilled Qwen、Llama和Gemma的273个检查点数据集上，z-sum分离了57个公开去拒绝模型和37个良性微调/合并/指令微调模型，AUROC 0.95，显著高于单一信号（0.84,0.90）。Youden校准阈值在保留族上平衡准确率0.89（FPR 0.11），仅漏检4/57个。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种有效的分诊工具，但并非防篡改方案：依赖已认证的参考锚点，且其声明受限于评估所用的注册数据集。存在两种失败模式：欺骗参考（无需训练可逃避检测）和白盒攻击（训练后的检查点虽超过阈值但仍保持不安全且连贯）。

Abstract: Can a platform tell, before deployment, whether an open-weight checkpoint has had its refusal mechanism stripped? Runtime guards cannot: they score generations, not the artifact. We combine two cheap internal signals, a reference-anchored activation refusal-gap and a weight-recovery energy of the base-to-candidate weight difference, into a threshold-free checkpoint audit. The two are negatively correlated and label-complementary: the gap supplies refusal-specificity and the weight energy supplies recall. On a 273-checkpoint registry spanning Qwen, DeepSeek-distilled Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, their z-sum separates 57 public abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes, merges, and instruction-tunes at AUROC 0.95, significantly above either signal alone (0.84, 0.90), and a Youden-calibrated threshold transfers to held-out families at balanced accuracy 0.89 (FPR 0.11), missing only 4 of 57. We then map two failures, in order of severity: a spoofed reference evades both axes with no training (ΔW=0, \r{ho}=1 by construction), and a white-box owner trains a checkpoint past the threshold while it stays guard-unsafe and coherent. The audit is effective triage, not tamper-proofing: it presumes an attested reference, and its claims are bounded by the registry we evaluate it on.

</details>


### [76] [Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02121)
*William Hackett,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个黑盒护栏侦察方法，通过监控HTTP、词汇和时序信号检测AI系统中是否存在护栏，并区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝，实验达到100%检测准确率和98%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在针对生产AI系统的黑盒对抗模拟中，研究人员难以区分护栏拦截与LLM自身拒绝，而这两种情况对应的绕过技术不同，影响攻击技术的选择和优化，亟需一种能识别护栏存在的方法。

Method: 提出黑盒护栏侦察方法，通过对目标AI系统的HTTP行为、词汇特征和时序信号进行行为监控来检测护栏存在，仅需黑盒访问且无需对护栏或AI系统的先验知识。

Result: 护栏检测准确率100%（良性/恶意交互行为分离统计显著，q<0.001）；区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝的平均F1分数达98%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效识别护栏存在、确定护栏设计屏蔽的内容类别，并可靠区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝，对AI安全评估和攻击技术优化有重要实践价值。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.

</details>


### [77] [Cloak and Detonate: Scanner Evasion and Dynamic Detection of Agent Skill Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02357)
*Zimo Ji,Congying Xu,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Xin Wei,Shuai Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首先揭示现有LLM编码代理技能扫描器易受规避攻击，提出SkillCloak框架（结构混淆+SFS打包）成功绕过8种扫描器（成功率>80-96%）；进而提出行为中心化运行时审计器SkillDetonate，通过沙箱执行和OS级信息流分析实现97%攻击检测率（2%假阳性率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理依赖第三方技能市场，存在软件供应链攻击面（凭证窃取、代码泄露、后门安装），而现有基于静态扫描的防御方案（模式匹配或LLM-as-judge）是否能够抵御自适应规避攻击尚不明确。

Method: 1) SkillCloak规避框架：结构混淆（语义等效重写可见载荷指标）+ SFS打包（安装时隐藏恶意组件，运行时恢复）。2) SkillDetonate运行时审计器：沙箱执行+按需闭包提升（观察运行时物化指令）+标记基污染分析（跨代理上下文、文件、进程、网络追踪敏感数据流）。

Result: SkillCloak的SFS打包绕过所有8种扫描器成功率超90%，结构混淆绕过大多数静态扫描器超80%、混合扫描器达96%。SkillDetonate检测97%攻击（2%假阳性率），对真实恶意技能维持87%检测率。

Conclusion: 基于外观的静态审计不足以防御恶意技能，需要转向行为中心的运行时审计方法。

Abstract: LLM coding agents increasingly rely on third-party agent skills from public marketplaces, which execute with the agent's privileges and create a software supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill can steal credentials, exfiltrate source code, or install backdoors. Existing defenses use static skill scanners based on pattern matching or LLM-as-judge analysis, but it remains unclear whether they withstand adaptive evasions that preserve malicious behavior while changing payload appearance.
  This paper first presents an adversarial study of existing skill scanners through SkillCloak, a payload-preserving evasion framework that keeps the attack semantics intact while transforming their visible form. SkillCloak uses two complementary strategies: Structural Obfuscation, which rewrites visible payload indicators into semantically equivalent forms, and Self-Extracting Skill (SFS) Packing, which hides malicious components from the install-time view and restores them during agent execution. Across eight scanners and 1,613 in-the-wild malicious skills, SFS Packing bypasses every scanner at over 90%, while Structural Obfuscation bypasses over 80% on most static scanners and reaches 96% on a hybrid scanner, showing that appearance-based auditing is insufficient.
  Motivated by this finding, we propose SkillDetonate, a behavior-centric runtime auditor that executes skills in a sandbox and detects malicious effects through OS-boundary information-flow evidence rather than install-time appearance. SkillDetonate combines on-demand closure lift, which observes instructions materialized during execution, with marker-based taint analysis, which tracks sensitive-data flows across the agent context, files, processes, and network operations. The results show that SkillDetonate detects 97% of attacks at a 2% false-positive rate and sustains 87% detection on real-world malicious skills.

</details>


### [78] [SoK: A Taxonomy for Cybersecurity Incident Response Influence Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02451)
*Thomas Biege,Marius Brockhoff,Jonas Kaspereit,Fabian Ising,Lea Gröber,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物，构建了"网络安全事件响应影响因素分类法(CIR-IF Taxonomy)"，涵盖了1999年至2024年中期的研究成果。该分类法与七个既有科学框架及NIST SP 800-61r3事件响应框架对比，结果证明其提供了更丰富、更严谨、更系统化的影响因素组织视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件响应领域的文献正在快速增长，但缺乏一个统一框架来系统组织已有的研究成果。事件响应涉及技术、人机交互、组织理论、人为因素等多个领域，需要一个综合性的整合视角来帮助研究者识别未被充分探索的领域，从而更有效地开展实证和理论研究。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，对417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物进行分析，构建了CIR-IF Taxonomy分类法；随后将分类法中的类别与七个既有科学框架及NIST SP 800-61r3事件响应框架中的要素进行系统比较。

Result: CIR-IF Taxonomy相较于现有框架，提供了更丰富、更严谨、更系统化的事件响应影响因素组织视图。

Conclusion: 该分类法能够有效整合多领域分散知识，为研究者识别研究空白和针对性开展研究提供了结构化工具。

Abstract: Cybersecurity incident response has emerged as a critical area of interest for both researchers and practitioners. The corpus of literature on cybersecurity incident response is expanding, yet a unified framework for systematically organizing the accumulated knowledge remains absent. The aspects of incident response span multiple domains, including technology, human-computer interaction, organizational theory, and human factors. A comprehensive, integrative perspective on these factors can enable researchers to identify underexplored areas and more effectively target their empirical and theoretical investigations. Our study systematizes the factors that influence organizational preparedness for and response to cybersecurity incidents. Through a systematic review of academic literature (n = 417) and non-scientific publications (n = 40), we derived the "Cybersecurity Incident Response Influencing Factor Taxonomy" (\textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy}). Existing empirical findings were classified within this taxonomy, providing a comprehensive and up-to-date overview of knowledge from the period 1999 to mid-2024. The taxonomy categories were systematically compared with seven established scientific frameworks and with the \textit{NIST Cyber Security Framework} elements referenced in the \textit{NIST Special Publication 800-61r3} incident response profile. The results of this comparison show that the \textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy} delivers a richer, more rigorous, and more systematically organized view of the factors that drive and shape incident response.

</details>


### [79] [Embedding Inference Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01276)
*Cedric Fitiavana Raelijohn,Sébastien Gambs,Jean-Francois Rajotte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅能获取检索文档集合（无排序和分数）的黑盒设置下，如何通过精心设计的查询从候选模型集中识别出目标嵌入模型（即嵌入推理攻击EIA），并验证了该方法在含重排序器及真实RAG系统中的有效性，同时提出了相似度阈值等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入逆向攻击通常假设攻击者已知目标嵌入模型，但实际IR系统往往以黑盒形式提供服务，攻击者仅能看到检索结果的无序集合。本文旨在探索在这种更严格的黑盒约束下，是否仍能识别出后端使用的具体嵌入模型，从而揭示信息检索系统的安全隐患。

Method: 1) 构造判别性查询：针对候选嵌入模型集合，设计能够产生差异化检索结果的查询。2) 黑盒推断：仅基于检索返回的无序文档集合（无排名或相似度分数）进行模型归属判定。3) 在含重排序器的系统中验证攻击有效性。4) 在真实RAG系统上测试，查询需设计为能绕过LLM对非标准问题的拒答机制。5) 提出并评估相似度阈值等防御策略。

Result: 在黑盒设置下，仅通过观察无序检索文档集合，攻击者能够有效从候选模型中识别出目标嵌入模型。该方法在包含重排序器的系统中仍具有判别能力。在真实RAG系统中，精心设计的查询可以绕过LLM的输入过滤机制，成功实施攻击。提出的相似度阈值等缓解策略能够提供一定防御效果。

Conclusion: 即使信息检索系统仅暴露最有限的检索结果（无序文档集合），且即使存在重排序器或RAG等防御组件，嵌入模型的身份仍可能被攻击者推断出来。这揭示了当前IR系统在模型隐私方面存在新的安全风险，需要开发更有效的防御机制。

Abstract: Embedding models are essential components of modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems, yet they are typically hidden behind APIs. Recent works have shown that dense IR system can lead to security vulnerabilities such as embedding inversion attacks. However, such attacks usually require that the attacker knows the embedding model for the attack to be applicable. In this paper, we study IR systems under a black-box setting in which the adversary observes only the unordered set of retrieved documents, without ranking or similarity scores. We demonstrate that in such contexts, tailored queries allow an adversary to identify which embedding model is in use from a set of known model candidate, which we coin as an embedding inference attack (EIA). We also show that certain queries remain discriminative even when the system includes a reranker as a potential defense mechanism. We further validate our method on a real Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system, in which the tailored queries bypass the LLM's tendency to reject inputs it does not recognize as well-formed questions. Finally, we propose and evaluate other mitigation strategies such as similarity thresholds.

</details>

### [80] [The General Stability of Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01546)
*Houming Chen,H. V. Jagadish*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于距离的通用稳定性（general stability）框架，用于评估加权评分排名在权重变化下的稳健性，并给出了相应的算法（二维扫描算法、无偏多维采样器及针对拟凸距离函数的Conv-SC算法），实验证明了方法的有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精确稳定性要求排名完全不变，过于严苛——权重微小扰动导致排名微调（如交换两个低排名项）即被计为不稳定。本文希望引入距离度量，让用户自定义哪些排名变化是重要的，从而更灵活、更现实地评估排名稳定性。

Method: (1) 形式化定义通用稳定性（general stability），用用户定义的排名距离函数聚合权重空间区域；(2) 提出二维扫描算法和无偏多维采样器，扩展精确稳定性计算方法；(3) 识别拟凸距离函数为可处理子类，提出Conv-SC算法，将稳定性计算转化为凸体积近似问题，可利用随机多项式时间方法求解。

Result: 在8个真实数据集和生成实例上的实验表明：距离敏感的稳定性指标能提供有信息量的结果；所提估计器准确且实用；Conv-SC在高维拟凸距离函数下显著提升了扩展性。

Conclusion: 通用稳定性框架比精确稳定性更灵活、更具应用意义，拟凸距离子类使得高维稳定性计算在多项式时间内可处理，为排名稳健性分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Rankings derived from weighted scoring functions are widely used in settings such as university rankings and employment candidate evaluations. Since ranking weights are often chosen by organizations or analysts, ranking stability asks whether a reported ranking persists under reasonable weight changes. Prior work on stable rankings formalizes this idea through volume-based stability, which measures the fraction of the weight space that induces the target ranking exactly.
  This exact-match requirement can be too blunt: once a perturbed weight vector produces a different ranking, exact stability gives it no credit, whether the change replaces the top-ranked item or only swaps two nearly tied lower-ranked items. We propose general stability, a distance-based generalization that aggregates ranking regions according to a user-defined distance from the target ranking. This lets users specify which ranking changes matter in the application, while recovering exact stability as a special case.
  Our algorithmic focus is stability computation: given a reported or user-specified ranking and a distance function, estimate its general-stability score. We give a two-dimensional sweep algorithm and an unbiased multidimensional sampler that extend exact-stability methods, and analyze why sampling can scale poorly as the dimension grows. Motivated by this scaling challenge, we identify quasiconvex distance functions as a tractable subclass and introduce Conv-SC, which reduces stability computation for this subclass to convex-volume approximation, where randomized polynomial-time methods are available. Experiments on eight real datasets and generated instances show that distance-sensitive stability gives informative real-data results, that our estimators are accurate and practical, and that Conv-SC improves scaling with dimension for quasiconvex distance functions.

</details>


### [81] [pykci: A Compact Urban Knowledge Graph for Semantic and Spatial Queries using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01605)
*Huynh Duc An Son Nguyen,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: pykci 是一个开源系统，可将 CityGML 2.0 数据集转换为 Neo4j 知识图谱，并通过自然语言查询（text-to-Cypher）实现对语义 3D 城市模型的交互，支持空间索引、3D Tiles 可视化及无损回导出 CityGML，以汉堡 LoD2 数据展示了复杂空间语义查询（如识别适合绿化的屋顶表面）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CityGML 的 XML 编码和关系映射查询困难，需要专业知识，阻碍了城市规划者、GIS 从业者和公民直接利用丰富的语义 3D 城市模型数据。

Method: 构建了一个完整的端到端 Python 流水线：将 CityGML 数据集转换为 Neo4j 知识图谱（含 R-tree 空间索引），通过模型无关的 text-to-Cypher 机制（将图谱模式作为上下文，由 LLM 将自然语言翻译为 Cypher 查询）实现查询，并支持 OGC 3D Tiles 可视化和无损回导出。

Result: 在德国汉堡的开放政府 CityGML LoD2 数据集上验证了系统能力，展示了复杂语义和空间查询（如识别适合绿化的屋顶表面），支持本地开源模型和商业模型两种 LLM 方案，答案基于精确城市数据而非模型参数记忆，降低了幻觉风险。

Conclusion: pykci 使得非专家用户无需掌握查询语言和数据库模式即可交互查询语义 3D 城市模型，为城市规划和公众参与提供了可审计、低幻觉的数据访问方式。

Abstract: CityGML, the OGC standard for modeling, storage, and exchange of semantic 3D city models, describes urban objects with detailed semantics, geometry, and topology. Yet this richness is difficult to query directly: CityGML's XML encoding is designed for exchange rather than analysis, and relational mappings expose it through schemas requiring expert knowledge. We present pykci (Python Knowledge Graph for Cities), an open-source system that transforms CityGML 2.0 datasets into a compact urban knowledge graph in Neo4j and makes it queryable in natural language. The graph schema covers all thematic feature modules of CityGML 2.0 across all levels of detail and is spatially indexed with an R-tree for efficient geometric retrieval. A complete end-to-end Python pipeline ingests CityGML datasets into the knowledge graph, exports them to OGC 3D Tiles for interactive visualization, and supports lossless round-trip export of all content back to CityGML. For querying, the graph is paired with a large language model through a model-agnostic text-to-Cypher mechanism: the graph schema is supplied as context, and the model translates natural-language questions into Cypher queries executed against the graph. We evaluate both a locally running open-weight model, which keeps sensitive city data on-premise, and a state-of-the-art commercial model for the most demanding spatial and semantic queries. Answers are grounded in exact city data rather than the model's parametric memory, reducing hallucination and providing auditable provenance for every response. We demonstrate the system on open-government CityGML LoD2 datasets from Hamburg, Germany, including complex semantic and spatial queries such as identifying roof surfaces suitable for greening. pykci enables urban planners, GIS practitioners, and citizens to interact with semantic 3D city models without expertise in query languages and database schemas.

</details>


### [82] [AgenticDataBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01647)
*Zhaoyan Sun,Shan Zhong,Daizhou Wen,Jiaxing Han,Guoliang Li,Ying Yan,Peng Zhang,Yu Su,Xiang Qi,Baolin Sun,Chengyuan Yang,Tao Fang,Huaiyu Ruan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AgenticDataBench是一个用于评估基于大语言模型的数据智能体的综合性基准，涵盖15个垂直领域（含5个真实B2B用例），通过数据科学技能量化覆盖范围，并提出基于LLM的系统化任务生成方法，提供了细粒度的技能级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学领域缺乏能够全面、细粒度评估基于LLM的数据智能体的基准，现有评估无法捕捉数据科学工作流的多样性和复杂性。

Method: 1) 从15个垂直领域收集真实数据集和任务，包括5个金融科技B2B用例；2) 引入数据科学技能（重复性数据操作模式），通过Stack Overflow大规模任务解决方案的技能对齐层次聚类提取代表性技能；3) 对真实业务任务，选择技能组成多样性最大的任务-解决方案对；4) 对缺乏真实任务的领域，提出基于LLM的系统化任务生成方法；5) 基于标注的基准和开源测试平台评估现有数据智能体。

Result: 构建了覆盖15个垂直领域的综合基准，包含真实B2B用例，通过数据科学技能量化覆盖范围，并基于此评估了当前最先进的数据智能体，提供了详细的技能级洞察。

Conclusion: AgenticDataBench为数据科学自动化领域的LLM智能体评估提供了更全面、细粒度的基准，有助于推动该领域的研究与发展。

Abstract: Data science aims to derive actionable insights from heterogeneous raw data, unlocking the value of the massive amounts of data generated in modern society. Automating this process is essential to reducing labor-intensive efforts for data scientists and enabling scalable data-driven applications. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based data agents have emerged as a promising solution to automate data science workflows. However, the field lacks comprehensive benchmarks to rigorously evaluate these agents across diverse scenarios with fine-grained granularity. To address this gap, we propose AgenticDataBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring realistic tasks spanning diverse domains with fine-grained ground-truth labels. This enables evaluations to capture the diversity and complexity of data science workflows and the detailed performance of agents. First, to cover diverse domains, we collect real datasets and tasks from 15 vertical domains, including 5 real-world B2B use cases from a leading fintech company. Second, to remove redundancy in real-world tasks and generate high-quality tasks for domains lacking real data, we introduce data science skills, recurring data-centric operational patterns, and quantify benchmark coverage by the number of skills included. Representative skills are extracted from large-scale task solutions on Stack Overflow using skill-aligned hierarchical clustering. Third, for real-world business tasks, we select task-solution pairs that maximize diversity in skill composition, ensuring broad coverage of practical scenarios. Fourth, to generate realistic tasks for devise domains without real tasks, we propose a systematic LLM-based task generation approach to create workflows and tasks based on these skills. Finally, we evaluate state-of-the-art data agents using our annotated benchmark and open-sourced testbed, providing detailed skill-level insights.

</details>


### [83] [HNSW with Accuracy Guarantees Using Graph Spanners -- A Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02338)
*Minghao Li,Raghav Mittal,Sanjivni Rana,Suraj Shetiya,Gautam Das,Nick Koudas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出“先验证后纠正”（Certify-then-Rectify）框架，通过在HNSW启发式搜索基础上引入无分布统计验证器动态评估搜索结果质量，仅在需要时升级为精确恢复算法，从而在保持HNSW平均速度的同时保证最坏情况下的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNSW图作为行业标准具有对数复杂度和优异经验性能，但其依赖的贪心图遍历是启发式方法，缺乏正确性的理论保证。现有方法要么牺牲速度追求严格性，要么接受启发式的潜在错误，二者之间存在空白。

Method: 提出“Certify-then-Rectify”两级框架：1）使用无分布统计验证器以极低开销动态评估标准HNSW搜索结果质量；2）当认证结果质量低时，安全升级到精确恢复算法。为降低精确恢复计算成本，将HNSW图重新解释为几何spanner，并利用极值理论（Extreme Value Theory）随机估计其最大经验伸展因子，从而在数学上界定真实最近邻的最大距离。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，所提出的分层框架能够实现HNSW的平均速度，同时保证精确搜索的最坏情况正确性，并优于其他适用方法。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了启发式搜索速度与精确检索严谨性之间的鸿沟，为近似最近邻搜索提供了兼具效率与可保证正确性的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graphs serve as the industry standard due to their logarithmic complexity and strong empirical performance. However, HNSW relies on greedy graph traversal, a heuristic that provides no theoretical guarantees of correctness. In this paper, we propose a novel "Certify-then-Rectify" framework that bridges the gap between the speed of heuristic search and the rigor of exact retrieval. Rather than discarding HNSW, our approach first employs a distribution-free statistical certifier to dynamically evaluate the quality of a standard HNSW search with minimal overhead. If certification indicates that the retrieved neighbors are of low quality, the framework safely escalates to a rigorous exact recovery algorithm. To make this exact recovery computationally feasible, we reinterpret the HNSW graph as a geometric spanner and utilize Extreme Value Theory to stochastically estimate its maximum empirical stretch factor. This allows us to mathematically bound the maximum distance of true nearest neighbors. Extensive evaluations on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our tiered framework delivers the average-case speed of HNSW while ensuring the worst-case correctness of exact search and outperforming other applicable approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），将认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与深度学习神经网络相结合，通过Q矩阵约束技能-项目关系，确保潜在掌握特征的可解释性，并设计了带L2惩罚的损失函数以平衡预测性能与结构对齐，开发了可解释对齐指标进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有结构可解释性但灵活性不足，而深度神经网络虽预测能力强但缺乏心理测量透明度，两者之间存在鸿沟，需要一种既能保持认知理论一致性又能利用神经网络灵活性的方法。

Method: M-QCDNet将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络中，约束项目-技能关系；提出带L2惩罚的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不对齐的技能进行惩罚；开发了可解释对齐指标用于量化预测技能激活与项目级别技能的一致性。

Result: 模型保持了潜在掌握特征的可解释性并与认知理论一致，在预测性能与结构对齐之间取得了平衡，可解释对齐指标有效量化了技能激活的一致性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet弥合了心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，支持早期学习困难检测和掌握导向的干预，推动了面向认知诊断的可解释、公平且可操作的人工智能发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [85] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IIRMAN（Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network）的跨被试脑电应激检测方法，通过在各频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，结合频率聚类聚合和切片内-间注意力机制，在三个数据集上达到82.78%的平衡准确率，仅需1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电应激检测中，判别性应激相关模式既依赖被试个体又具有频率特异性。传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了高频认知状态解码至关重要的神经振荡信息；而标准时间分词化常破坏切片间的时间连贯性。

Method: 1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间；2) 频率聚类聚合（Frequency Cluster Aggregation）选择信息性频谱成分，形成与脑电节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇；3) 切片内-间注意力模块（Intra-Inter Slice Attention）自适应整合局部切片级频谱动力学与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上一致优于五个SOTA基线，最高平衡准确率82.78%，参数量仅1.60M，FLOPs为31.95M。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了跨被试脑电应激检测中的个体差异和频率特异性问题，在保持高效计算的同时取得了优异性能。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [86] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程示例系统（PBE）在面对**对抗性示例破坏**时的鲁棒性，而非传统随机噪声假设。作者形式化了固定集最坏情况破坏问题，提出**版本空间分区聚合（VPA）**防御方法，并通过实验证明了低边际PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，而VPA仅在清洁语义保持分区投票边际时有效——这一边界在现实任务中常被突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PBE系统假设示例错误来自随机噪声并最小化期望损失，但忽视了**对抗性攻击者**这一更危险的失败模式——攻击者观察合成器后选择性地破坏示例，最大化程序输出的损害。本文旨在揭示这一对抗鲁棒性维度并提供防御分析。

Method: 1) 形式化**固定集最坏情况破坏**问题（针对有限PBE版本空间）；2) 实现**精确界内池搜索**和**启发式破坏搜索**（针对字符串变换DSL）；3) 提出**版本空间分区聚合（VPA）**：在不相交示例组上分别合成，然后通过语义签名投票；4) 在多种数据集上评估：人工策划/生成的DSL任务、SyGuS PBE_SLIA片段、SYNTRA Playgol v2、以及噪声PBE基线。

Result: - 一次精心策划的编辑即可翻转全部8个尖峰任务，而200次随机控制（typo、DSL池、距离匹配随机）仅成功10.3%、11.0%、16.7%；- 生成边际为1的示例在预算1下被翻转，但VPA可恢复；- 在公共SyGuS上，投票边际接近1，自适应攻击者可将VPA准确率降至零；- Playgol上VPA与typo及同池随机控制相比呈现正向配对引导差距；- 基于20个受控边际1任务的小规模LLM提示实验显示了相同的清洁→攻击模式。

Conclusion: 核心主张是有意**边界性且部分否定性**的：低边际PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，这是随机错误和噪声PBE评估所忽视的；**语义分区聚合防御仅在清洁语义保持分区投票边际时有效，而这在现实任务中常常失效**。研究为PBE系统对抗鲁棒性的理论边界和实践局限提供了系统性证据。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [87] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)
*Abdullah Al Tasim,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段学习管道：用注意力增强门控循环网络从机载运动学与动力学估计局部风矢量，再将该估计输入PPO强化学习控制器。风估计器在冯·卡门湍流中训练，水平风RMSE达0.40 m/s、方向误差3.2°，接近不可解析湍流引起的误差下限。RL控制器相较无视风的PD基线，在4-12 m/s平均风下水平轨迹跟踪误差降低48%，在全部评估回合中胜出。消融实验将改进分解为运动学分量和风感知分量：风感知贡献随风速增大，从微风时较小升至强风时约占总收益一半。控制器在13-15 m/s分布外风况下仍能优雅退化，而基线完全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型多旋翼飞行器在边界层湍流风中轨迹跟踪精度下降，传统反馈控制在风速与空速相当的情况下可能失效，需要将风感知信息融入控制策略以提升鲁棒性。

Method: 两阶段学习管道：第一阶段采用注意力增强门控循环网络，以机载IMU、GPS等运动学与动力学数据作为输入，在模拟的冯·卡门湍流（含幂律风切变和偏转）中训练风估计器；第二阶段将冻结的风估计器输出作为观测输入给PPO强化学习控制器进行轨迹跟踪控制。

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下水平风RMSE 0.40 m/s、方向误差3.2°，垂直爬升廓线技能评分0.861；PPO控制器在4-12 m/s风下轨迹跟踪误差较PD基线降低48%，100%评估回合胜出。消融实验显示风感知贡献从微风到强风渐增至约一半收益。控制器在13-15 m/s分布外风下仍可优雅退化。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的风估计与强化学习控制相结合，可显著提升多旋翼在湍流环境中的轨迹跟踪性能，且系统对未见过的强风具有较好的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>


### [88] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识图卷积网络的心电图识别方法，利用PRQST关键点构建双流有向图（空间+时间），在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上以88.1%平均F1和76.3%罕见类别F1超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医疗等专业领域（如ECG识别）中可解释性不足，传统端到端卷积神经网络难以融入领域知识。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络：提取PRQST关键点作为领域知识，构建双流有向图——空间有向图建模关键点间位置关系，时间有向图建模相邻周期间时序依赖。

Result: 在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上，九分类任务平均F1为88.1%，罕见类别平均F1为76.3%，均优于现有最佳模型。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识（PRQST关键点）可有效提升ECG识别性能，尤其对罕见类别的检测效果改善显著。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [89] [Predicting Closed-Loop Performance of Latent World Models: Offline Checkpoint Selection for MPC and Model-Based RL Under Non-Markovian Rewards in LunarLander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01736)
*Nikolai Smolyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一套基于最优控制理论的验证时诊断指标，用于预测潜世界模型的闭环性能。发现奖励可观测性分数(ROF)是最强单指标，并组合成复合奖励可观测性分数(CROF)用于模型检查点选择。CROF选出的模型在LunarLander任务上比无模型A2C基线高出约24.5分，同时减少约65倍的真实环境交互次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练潜世界模型时，验证损失和多步预测RMSE在闭环性能崩溃后仍在改善，导致难以选择合适的检查点。需要能从离线验证诊断中预测下游闭环性能的指标。

Method: 从最优控制理论出发设计结构化的验证时诊断指标，在Gymnasium的LunarLander v3（具有塑形奖励）上训练RSSM世界模型，以CEM-MPC回报作为闭环质量基准。评估40个指标，发现奖励可观测性分数(ROF)最强。将ROF与三个结构化正则化项组合为CROF分数。

Result: CROF选择的世界模型训练的基于模型的A2C策略比无模型A2C基线高约24.5个回报点，同时减少了约65倍的真实环境交互。该世界模型也能驱动强大的零样本CEM-MPC策略。

Conclusion: 验证时诊断指标（特别是ROF和CROF）能有效预测世界模型的闭环性能，为离线检查点选择提供可靠依据，显著提升样本效率。

Abstract: We study how to predict the downstream closed-loop performance of a learned latent world model from validation-time diagnostics alone. Choosing the right checkpoint from a world-model training run is difficult: validation loss and multi-step prediction RMSE keep improving long after closed-loop performance has collapsed. We present a suite of structural validation-time diagnostics drawn from optimal-control theory and apply them to Gymnasium's LunarLander v3, which features shaped rewards. We train an RSSM [5, 4] world model on it and treat per checkpoint CEM-MPC return as the oracle for closed-loop quality. By evaluating 40 metrics against this oracle, we find that the strongest single predictor is the Reward Observability Fraction (ROF), which measures the reward predictor's dependence on the observable subspace. We combine ROF with three structural regularizers into a single-number offline checkpoint selection score, the Composite Reward Observability Fraction (CROF). The CROF-selected world model trains a model-based A2C policy that beats a fairly evaluated model-free A2C baseline by ~24.5 return points while using ~65x fewer real-environment interactions, and the same world model also drives a strong zero-shot CEM-MPC policy. Code and data: https://github.com/nsmoly/LunarLander_RSSM.

</details>


### [90] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的性能，发现在GloVe嵌入族上存在维度缩放交叉现象：该算法保持近恒定的维度缩放指数，而其他方法（图、树、分区）的吞吐量随维度增加而退化。该算法具有近线性查询缩放、更低索引成本的优势，在重建频繁或高维场景下具有竞争力，并对高效Transformer架构的成本分析有指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的近似最近邻搜索方法在现代规模化分析中被忽视，缺乏对其在高维和大规模数据集下性能的系统性表征。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统性实验表征，分析其随数据集规模N和维度d的缩放行为，并与图、树、分区等其他ANN方法进行对比。

Result: 发现了维度缩放交叉现象：多探针网格算法保持近恒定的维度缩放指数，而其他方法吞吐量随维度增加而退化；查询缩放接近线性；索引成本低于其他ANN方法。

Conclusion: 多探针网格算法在重建频繁或高维场景下具有竞争力；其N和d缩放特性可用于指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析，因为自注意力已被形式化为ANN操作。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [91] [A Memory Efficient Unified Algorithm for Online Learning of Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02050)
*Yuval Ran-Milo,Angelos Assos,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线预测线性动力系统（LDS）的稳定性问题，提出一种仅需 $\widetilde{O}(k)$ 个可学习参数的自适应算法，其中 $k$ 代表不稳定复杂度（实稳定区间外的特征值及非半单模态数量），并证明 $k$ 是滤波器预测器的必要下界。实验表明该方法在高维系统中以同等参数预算显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中稳定化未知线性动力系统是极具挑战性的问题，而在线预测是其自然前提。现有方法通常需要与全隐藏状态维度相当的 memory 开销，这在实际高维系统中不可行。作者旨在开发一种 memory 开销仅依赖系统内在复杂度（而非全维度）的预测算法，并探索该复杂度究竟应为多少。

Method: 1) 定义不稳定复杂度 $k$：实稳定区间外的特征值（不快速衰减）以及非半单模态的数量；2) 证明高 $k$ 系统无法不经指数级控制代价而稳定；3) 提出统一在线算法，参数规模为 $\widetilde{O}(k)$，适用于任意 LDS（包括不可对角化、复模态或发散模态）；4) 给出下界证明：任何基于滤波器的预测器至少需要 $k$ 个滤波器。

Result: 1) 低不稳定复杂度是稳定化有意义的必要条件；2) 所提算法以 $\widetilde{O}(k)$ 参数实现亚线性遗憾；3) 滤波器预测器的参数下界为 $k$；4) 实验在高维系统中以同等参数显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: $k$ 是 LDS 在线预测问题的正确复杂度度量。对于低不稳定复杂度的系统，本文给出了高效的参数自适应在线预测算法，为后续稳定化控制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Motivated by the challenge of stabilizing a general unknown linear dynamical system (LDS) from observations, we study the natural prerequisite of online prediction. Our goal is to achieve sublinear regret with a memory footprint that adapts to the intrinsic complexity of the dynamics rather than the full hidden -- state dimension. We focus on the practically central regime of systems with low instability complexity -- eigenvalues outside the real stable interval that do not decay rapidly, together with non-semisimple modes-potentially embedded in an otherwise stable real spectrum of much higher dimension; we write $k$ for this count. This regime is the primary setting in which stabilization is plausible: we show that many systems with high instability complexity cannot be stabilized without exponentially large controls. Thus, prediction is meaningful for stabilization precisely when the instability complexity is small. Within this regime, we introduce a unified online algorithm that handles every LDS (including non-diagonalizable systems with complex or exploding modes) with a learnable parameter count of $\widetilde{O}(k)$. Finally, we prove a lower bound showing that $k$ is a valid complexity measure: any filter-based predictor needs at least $k$ filters. Experiments corroborate our theory: on a high-dimensional system, our predictor sharply outperforms prior methods at an equal parameter budget.

</details>


### [92] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IonSense-QKG框架，通过为锂离子电池公开数据集添加量子相关元数据（包括任务类型、传感模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围及NISQ可行性），引入透明量子就绪度评分来排序数据集，支持基于查询的数据集发现，为近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流提供数据集选择启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构、访问状态和预处理复杂度等方面存在显著差异，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，但缺乏系统性的量子就绪度评估框架。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，构建量子就绪元数据框架，为公开电池数据集记录添加量子相关元数据，引入透明量子就绪度评分作为数据集选择启发式方法，并通过基于查询的发现机制验证框架有效性。

Result: 实现了基于查询的元数据发现，可识别适用于紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集；发布了包含元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的完整工件。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择重新定位为数据管理问题，为数据驱动的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，其评分旨在作为数据集选择启发式方法而非量子优势的证据。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [93] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL，一种通过强化学习为扩散模型采样学习自适应时间步分配的方法，在保持其他采样流程不变的情况下，仅通过改变时间步网格即可持续提升样本质量，且学到的调度具有跨预算、数据集、求解器等的广泛泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样的时间步分配（timestep allocation）通常依赖均匀网格或人工设计的固定调度，这些方法缺乏适应性，可能导致次优的采样质量。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一种能够自适应学习时间步分配的控制方法。

Method: 提出ART（Adaptive Reparameterized Time）连续时间控制框架，将采样时钟速度作为控制变量，学习时间变换使得均匀网格对应自适应时间步。基于领先阶欧拉误差代理构建优化目标。进一步提出ART-RL，引入辅助随机化（高斯策略）将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，并推导出可实现的演员-评论员更新算法。

Result: 从低维控制实验到图像生成任务中，ART-RL仅改变时间步网格即可持续提升采样质量，优于强基线调度。学到的调度在采样预算、数据集、求解器、流水线和表示空间之间表现出广泛的泛化能力，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 证明了ART-RL与ART在优化器层面等价（最优高斯策略的均值恢复最优时间翘曲率），并建立了策略评估与改进的特征刻画。该方法可即插即用，为扩散模型采样提供了一种通用的自适应时间步分配方案。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [94] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的DNA甲基化分类方法，在CNS肿瘤分类任务上，在参考队列（96%准确率）和独立临床队列（91类水平86%、家族水平93%准确率）上均超越了现有最优方法（分别提升约4和5个百分点），具有临床意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DNA甲基化分析已成为CNS肿瘤分类的有力工具，但在跨队列迁移性、方法论正确性和稳健的多分类评估方面仍存在重要挑战，亟需更严谨的机器学习方法。

Method: 采用稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维，结合多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）进行分类，并在与广泛使用的参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 在2,801样本参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率96%；在1,104样本独立临床队列上，91类水平准确率86%，甲基化家族水平准确率93%，相比现有最优的82%和88%分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该模型基于更严谨的机器学习方法论，在所有评估设置中一致优于先前最优方法，能够实质性地提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，对临床诊断和治疗决策具有积极影响。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation.
