### [169] [Online Resource Allocation with Continuous Random Consumption: Regret under Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02196)
*Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究连续奖励和消耗量下的在线资源分配问题，提出了基于活跃加权质量指数的 regret 上下界，并设计了达到最优 regret（从 $O((\log T)^2)$ 到 $\Omega(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})$）的策略，无需流体非退化假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线资源分配研究多假设资源消耗量为单位大小或离散值，缺乏对连续分布下奖励和消耗量的统一处理框架，特别是当流体松弛退化和对偶不唯一时，理论结果尚不完善。

Method: 通过活跃加权质量指数 $p$ 刻画 cutoff 附近的请求质量分布特征，利用样本路径边际策略（sample-path marginal policy）实现自适应分配，并建立匹配的 regret 上下界。

Result: 当 $p>1$ 时，任何在线策略的 regret 下界为 $\Omega(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})$，所提策略达到该下界（至多多项对数因子）；当 $p=1$ 时，regret 为 $O((\log T)^2)$。独立均匀分布下，若 size 与 value-to-size 比独立则 $p=1$，若 size 与 reward 独立则 $p=2$。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了连续消耗量在线资源分配的理论，证明了无需流体非退化假设即可实现 $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret，覆盖了原始退化和对偶非唯一等困难情形。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation when both rewards and consumption sizes may be continuously distributed. Requests arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably under fixed resource capacities. Each request belongs to one of finitely many observable types; conditional on an observable request type, both the reward and the scalar size are random, and the realized size scales a fixed type-specific resource-consumption vector. The model allows the deterministic fluid relaxation to be degenerate.
  We show that additive regret is governed by the size-weighted mass of requests whose value-to-size ratios lie near the active acceptance cutoffs. We formalize this quantity through an active weighted-mass exponent p. When p > 1, this cutoff mass is thin, and the problem is genuinely hard: every online policy must incur regret of order at least $T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}$, and this holds for every p > 1. A sample-path marginal policy matches this lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors; and when p = 1, so that the mass grows linearly near the cutoff, it attains $O((\log T)^2)$ regret. For example, if the size and the value-to-size ratio are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 1; if instead the size and the reward are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 2. Thus the policy achieves $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret throughout this regularity class without any fluid non-degeneracy assumption, allowing both primal degeneracy and dual non-uniqueness.

</details>


### [170] [Self-explainable Operator Learning for Discovering Spatial Patterns in Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02203)
*Mojgan Alishiri,Amirhossein Arzani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自解释的算子学习框架，通过将算子学习重写为积分方程表示的广义函数线性模型的线性组合，利用积分方程的加法可分解性将输入域划分为子域并计算局部积分，实现模型对输入-输出关系的直接可解释性，无需外部解释工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算子学习模型基于神经网络架构，是不透明的黑箱模型，难以理解其预测背后的推理过程，限制了其在科学应用中的可信度。

Method: 将算子学习重构为积分方程表示的广义函数线性模型的线性组合；利用积分方程的加法可分解性将输入域划分为子域；计算局部积分以评估每个区域对最终预测的贡献；从而实现输入-输出区域直接关联的解释。

Result: 在血流和非定常空气动力学等流体问题的函数到标量及函数到函数映射任务上，模型优先关注具有强特征梯度的区域；与现有事后可解释性方法的定性对比一致，但本方法的可解释性内嵌于算子结构本身。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种数学上透明、物理上可解释的方法来揭示数据关系，通过实现更知情的物理系统数据驱动分析，提升机器学习在科学应用中的可信度。

Abstract: Operator learning has emerged as a powerful tool for modeling complex physical systems in functional spaces. However, their neural network-based architectures make them opaque models, obscuring the reasoning behind their predictions. In this work, we introduce a self-explainable operator learning framework that overcomes this challenge by reformulating operator learning as a linear combination of generalized functional linear models expressed through integral equations. Exploiting the additive decomposability of these integral equations, we divide the input domain into subdomains and compute localized integrals to evaluate the contribution of each region to the final prediction. This decomposition enables direct interpretability where the model explains both inputs and outputs by linking specific input regions to corresponding output patterns, thereby revealing which spatial features drive predictions. We demonstrate the framework on function-to-scalar and function-to-function mappings in fluid flow problems involving blood flow and unsteady aerodynamics. The results show that the operator most often prioritizes regions with strong feature gradients, providing physically meaningful insight into the model's decision-making process. Comparisons with established post-hoc explainability methods demonstrate qualitative agreement while highlighting the key advantage of the proposed approach: explainability is embedded directly within the operator structure itself and does not require an external tool. Therefore, our framework provides a mathematically transparent and physically interpretable approach to uncover relationships within data, fostering trust in machine learning for scientific applications by enabling more informed data-driven analysis of physical systems.

</details>


### [171] [HERMES: A Multi-Granularity Labeling Substrate for Pre-training Data Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02266)
*Ziyun Qiao,Yue Min,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERMES 提出了一种基于学习语义变换和三级残差向量量化的层次化数据标注系统，解决传统数据混合方法中标签系统固定粒度的问题，并通过预训练实验展示了不同粒度下混合策略效果的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合方法依赖预分组的标签系统（如来源、主题、扁平聚类），这些标签固定在一个语义轴和单一粒度上，改变分辨率需要重建标签。论文认为瓶颈在于标签系统而非混合器，因此需要一种可复用的、数据驱动的层次化标签体系。

Method: HERMES 包含 Learned Semantic Transform 和三级残差向量量化（R-VQ），将每个文档标注为一个由粗到细的编码，前缀长度控制粒度，最多支持约 13 万个单元。

Result: 在粗粒度上，HERMES 在标准聚类指标上与 KMeans 类方法持平；在 1B 参数、25B token 的预训练中，层次结构暴露了固定粒度无法测试的交互：某一前缀长度下，组合 Stage-2 规则对比使 16 任务能力宏观平均提升 +0.0253，而在更细粒度下同一规则优势消失（候选池缩小约 5 倍）。

Conclusion: HERMES 将数据混合设计从在固定标签集之间选择，转变为在可复用的、数据驱动的粒度层次中进行导航。

Abstract: Most data-mixing methods assume the corpus has already been partitioned into groups, and the choice of those groups determines what a mixer can express. Existing labels, including provenance, topic or format taxonomies, and flat embedding clusters, commit to one semantic axis at one granularity; changing the resolution rebuilds the labels. We argue the bottleneck is the label system, not the mixer, and provide a hierarchical one. HERMES is a data-derived labeling substrate: a Learned Semantic Transform followed by 3-stage residual vector quantization annotates each document once into a coarse-to-fine code whose prefix length controls granularity up to approximately 130k cells. At coarse granularity HERMES sits at a plateau with KMeans-family methods on standard clustering metrics, so the contribution is the substrate, not the clusterer. On 1B-parameter, 25B-token pre-training, the hierarchy exposes an interaction fixed-granularity pipelines cannot test: at one prefix length, a combined Stage-2 rule contrast, equal-subbucket coverage versus size-proportional within-bucket quality top-30%, lifts a 16-task capability macro-average by +0.0253; at the next finer level, the same rule loses its measurable edge as candidate pools contract approximately 5x. HERMES reframes data mixture design from choosing among fixed label sets to navigating a reusable, data-derived granularity hierarchy.

</details>


### [172] [Generalization in offline RL: The structure is more important than the amount of pessimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02288)
*Max Weltevrede,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Wendelin Böhmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在离线强化学习的上下文MDP中，过度悲观不会阻碍最优泛化，关键在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的内在对称性，并提出通过策略提取阶段的 consistency loss 应用数据增强比传统增强数据集训练更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为过度悲观会阻碍离线RL的泛化能力，本文试图澄清悲观主义与泛化之间的关系，指出真正影响泛化的是悲观结构是否保持最优解的内在对称性，而非悲观程度本身。

Method: 首先通过理论证明展示非对称的适度悲观值函数可能比过度悲观但对称的值函数泛化更差；然后基于理论分析，提出数据增强应通过策略提取阶段的 consistency loss 实现，而非传统做法（在增强数据集上进行常规离线训练）；最后在旋转对称的 reacher 环境中使用 IQL 和 CQL 进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明对称性比悲观程度更重要；实验验证了在旋转对称环境中，通过 consistency loss 在策略提取阶段应用数据增强比传统增强数据集训练效果更好。

Conclusion: 离线RL中悲观主义不会内在阻碍泛化，关键在于悲观结构是否保持对称性；数据增强的最佳应用方式是在策略提取时使用 consistency loss，而非增强训练数据集。

Abstract: While pessimism counteracts overestimation bias in offline reinforcement learning (RL), being overly conservative has been associated with hindering certain forms of generalization. However, in this paper we demonstrate that being overly pessimistic does not inherently prevent optimal generalization in contextual MDPs (CMDPs). Instead, we argue successful generalization depends not on the amount of pessimism, but whether the pessimistic structure respects the underlying symmetries of the optimal solution. We prove that a mildly pessimistic, non-symmetric value function can generalize worse than an overly pessimistic, symmetric one. In offline RL, the structure of the pessimism is determined by the structure of the dataset coverage. As such, enforcing a symmetric value function can be non-trivial, and might require techniques such as data augmentation (DA). Inspired by our theoretical results, we argue that DA can best be applied through a consistency loss during policy extraction, rather than the common practice of (regular) offline training on an augmented dataset. This is empirically validated using IQL and CQL on a rotationally symmetric reacher environment.

</details>


### [173] [Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02291)
*Ruihang Li,Mengde Xu,Shuyang Gu,Leigang Qu,Fuli Feng,Han Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布奖励（Distribution-wise Reward）的强化学习微调框架，通过子集替换策略高效估计分布奖励，并对模型后合并系数进行RL优化，有效解决了传统逐样本奖励导致的奖励攻击（reward hacking）和模式坍塌问题，在SiT和EDM2等生成模型上显著提升了FID指标和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉生成中的强化学习策略使用逐样本奖励函数，容易导致奖励攻击（reward hacking），表现为图像多样性下降和视觉异常。作者旨在通过分布级奖励替代样本级奖励，解决模式坍塌问题。

Method: 1）提出分布奖励（Distribution-wise Reward），基于生成样本集与真实数据分布的对齐程度进行评估；2）设计子集替换策略（Subset-replace Strategy），仅更新生成参考集的一个小子集以降低分布奖励的计算成本；3）应用RL优化后验模型合并系数（Post-hoc Model Merging Coefficients），缓解由SDE引入的训练-推理不一致性。

Result: SiT模型FID-50K从8.30降至5.77，EDM2模型从3.74降至3.52；定性评估表明在保持多样性的前提下提升了感知质量。

Conclusion: 分布奖励结合子集替换策略能够有效缓解奖励攻击和模式坍塌，后合并系数的RL优化进一步提升了训练-推理一致性，整体框架在生成质量与多样性之间取得了更好平衡。

Abstract: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>


### [174] [One More Time: Revisiting Neural Quantum States from a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02292)
*Juan Agustín Duque,Sergio García Heredia,Vinicius Hernandes,Eliška Greplová,Thomas Spriggs,Aaron Courville,Anna Dawid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO)，一种基于信任区域的神经量子态优化算法，通过将变分能量最小化视为策略梯度问题，在幅度通道裁剪概率比、在相位通道裁剪相位增量，避免矩阵求逆并实现样本复用，在自旋系统和15亿参数量级上均优于Adam、minSR和SPRING。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归神经量子态虽能实现精确独立采样，但其优化方法仍不完善：Adam忽略了函数空间几何，随机重配置虽有理论保证但计算昂贵且数值不稳定，亟需一种兼具可扩展性与理论保证的优化方法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化重新形式化为Born分布上的advantage策略梯度问题，引入信任区域优化：在幅度通道裁剪概率比（类似PPO），在相位通道裁剪相位增量，避免显式矩阵求逆，通过信任区域约束保证稳定更新，并实现样本在多次更新间复用。

Result: 在Ising和受挫J1-J2一维/二维自旋系统中，PWO在稳定性和实际收敛速度上均优于Adam、minSR和SPRING；成功将NQS优化扩展到15亿参数的RWKV-7模型，规模超出此前工作三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: PWO桥接了策略梯度与量子态优化的鸿沟，为大规模神经量子态训练提供了可扩展、稳定且理论有保证的算法，展示了将强化学习技术应用于量子多体问题的潜力。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [175] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自门控注意力（SGA），一种线性复杂度的即插即用注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵和输入依赖残差组件替代标准自注意力的查询-键投影，在保持时序预测性能的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力在时序预测中计算和内存复杂度呈二次增长，限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量场景下的应用；通过分析发现注意力图存在冗余模式，可利用此特性设计更高效的注意力机制。

Method: 提出SGA，用共享可学习矩阵捕获通用注意力模式，用输入依赖残差组件捕获输入特异性变化，避免查询-键投影计算，实现线性时间和得分矩阵内存复杂度。

Result: 在9个公开数据集上验证，SGA在多种预测骨干网络中相比标准自注意力和轻量变体，在保持竞争力预测性能的同时提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA是一种部署友好的高效注意力替代方案，在时序预测中实现了效率与性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [176] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL算法，通过强化学习让LLM学会将复杂问题分解为独立可实现的子模块并重组，将GPU Token成本降低约50倍，在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上超越标准RL基线，解决标准生成无法企及的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM通过重复采样（测试时计算）和强化学习（提高单次准确率）两种策略提升能力，但当基策略产生正确解的概率接近零时，两种方法均失效。因此需要一种新的范式，不依赖"更努力采样"，而是"降低任务难度"。

Method: 提出DecompRL，一种强化学习算法，显式学习将问题分解为更小、可独立实现的子模块（函数），并学习重组这些实现。通过重组k个模块的n个实现，获得k^n个候选解，将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上（Qwen 2.5 7B, Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 token时优于标准RL和多样性优化RL基线；GPU Token成本降低约50倍；能解决标准生成无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 模块化分解与重组是一种有效的替代范式，通过将搜索空间从"生成正确解"转变为"生成可组合的模块"，大幅降低了推理成本并扩展了LLM可解决的问题范围。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [177] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuFS框架，利用神经元激活模式代替传统输出级信号进行主动少样本学习样本选择，通过双标准策略确保多样性和挑战性，在推理和文本分类任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFSL方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵、外部嵌入语义相似度）选择样本，忽略了模型内部动态机制，无法精准定位模型的知识盲区。

Method: 提出NeuFS框架，包含：(1) 用神经元激活模式直接表示样本；(2) 双标准选择策略：基于神经元模式的多样性选择确保样本覆盖范围，通过量化神经元共识识别模型易产生幻觉的信息性挑战样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的推理和文本分类任务中，NeuFS超越现有AFSL基线方法；消融实验证明内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更有效、更原则化的选择信号。

Conclusion: 基于模型内部神经元动态的样本选择策略比传统输出级代理信号更优越，为主动少样本学习提供了更原则化的新范式。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [178] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QFedAgent，一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，用于多智能体活动识别。该框架通过变分量子电路融合模块对加速度计-陀螺仪交互进行量子态编码和纠缠，仅需72个量子旋转参数（对比传统MLP融合的33K参数），实现约10倍参数缩减。在OPPORTUNITY数据集上基于非独立同分布划分的实验达到97.7%平均测试准确率，证明参数高效量子融合与传统联邦基线具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在多智能体机器人感知应用中面临异构、非独立同分布多模态传感器数据导致的传统算法性能退化问题，且经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本。

Method: 提出QFedAgent混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，核心创新在于使用变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠机制建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，以极少量量子旋转参数替代传统MLP融合层。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集的非独立同分布划分下达到97.7%平均测试准确率，参数从33K降至72个量子参数，实现约10倍参数缩减。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合模块在保持与传统联邦基线竞争性能的同时，大幅降低了参数开销和通信成本，验证了量子计算在联邦学习多模态融合中的有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出极端自适应Transformer（Exformer），通过引入包含Local、Stride和Extreme三个稀疏组件的极端自适应注意力机制，显式建模正常事件与极端事件之间的时序依赖，在四个真实水文径流数据集上实现了优于现有基线模型的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中的极端事件虽罕见但影响重大，现有Transformer模型对所有时间点统一处理，容易低估稀有极端模式，亟需一种能显式建模极端事件依赖关系的预测框架。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏注意力组件：Local（捕获短期依赖）、Stride（捕获周期性依赖）、Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端径流模式间的事件感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中均优于当前最先进的基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力机制能够有效提升Transformer模型在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [180] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且网络连通性越高鲁棒性越强（联邦学习不劣于分散式学习）。基于理论提出MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有D-SSL方法面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但缺乏对不同框架在该挑战下鲁棒性的理论理解，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 对D-SSL框架在non-IID设置下进行严格理论分析，比较MIM与CL的鲁棒性；2) 分析网络连通性对鲁棒性的影响；3) 提出MAR损失——MIM目标与局部到全局对齐正则化的改进方法。

Result: 理论结果表明：MIM预训练比CL对异构数据更鲁棒；D-SSL鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而增强，FL不劣于DecL。实验验证了理论分析，并证实MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究为D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，并推出MAR损失作为理论的实际应用，展示了理论对算法设计的指导作用。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [181] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，利用神经元激活引导数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过策略自蒸馏实现无标注微调，在提升领域内性能的同时保持跨领域泛化并避免校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标注自进化方法（SFT/GRPO变体导致域外性能下降，基于奖励的在线RL膨胀校准误差）存在缺陷，且专业领域专家标注成本高昂，亟需一种无需真实标注、不依赖外部交互的微调方案。

Method: 利用内部神经元激活值指导训练数据筛选和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略蒸馏从教师分布中学习，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准上，提升领域内任务性能，保持跨领域泛化能力，缓解校准误差膨胀，优于现有无标注基线方法。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD适用于外部监督或在线交互成本高昂的场景，与依赖已标注轨迹的离线RL方法本质不同。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构化优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型中的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其在部分力监督场景下提升明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP社区在架构和数据集方面投入大量研究，但优化器选择（长期默认使用Adam及其变体）这一设计维度尚未被系统探索。

Method: 实现并系统比较了Muon、SOAP和混合SOAP-Muon三种矩阵结构化优化器，在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP架构上进行训练，与Adam基线对比。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam；Muon仅带来部分增益；在部分力监督条件下改善尤为显著。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，SOAP及SOAP-Muon可作为Adam的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [183] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD通过选择性采纳教师指导，利用反向KL重心目标在密集token级蒸馏中缓解特权信息泄露并保持探索能力，在科学推理任务上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏(OPSD)方法中，教师基于特权信息提供密集token级监督，导致学生模型过拟合域内模式、抑制探索、跨域泛化差，且存在特权信息泄露问题——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采纳教师指导。将学生导向教师与学生的反向KL重心目标（加权几何组合），利用两分布差异自适应控制每个token位置的混合程度，在拟合教师分布与保持学生自身推理能力间取得平衡。

Result: 理论证明DemoPSD可实现泄露衰减（缓解特权信息泄露）和探索保持（在密集token级蒸馏下保持探索能力）。在SciKnowEval四个科学领域上优于GRPO和SDPO，同时保持更高训练熵，并在OOD的GPQA基准上鲁棒泛化。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过自适应加权几何组合解决特权信息泄露问题，在保持探索能力的同时提升跨域泛化性能，为LLM推理训练提供了更稳健的自蒸馏范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [184] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"模糊函数编程"范式，通过4B编译器将自然语言规范编译为轻量级神经工件，由冻结的0.6B解释器执行，在匹配32B模型性能的同时大幅降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常编程任务（如日志告警、JSON修复、搜索结果排序）难以用规则实现，目前依赖大语言模型API，存在本地性、可复现性和成本问题。

Method: 提出Program-as-Weights（PAW）方法：训练4B编译器将自然语言规范编译为参数高效适配器（adapter），由冻结的0.6B轻量级解释器（Qwen3）执行。配套发布包含1000万样本的FuzzyBench数据集。

Result: PAW（0.6B解释器）匹配直接提示Qwen3-32B的性能，推理内存约为其1/50，在MacBook M3上达到30 tokens/s的推理速度。

Conclusion: 将基础模型从逐输入的问题求解器重新定位为工具构建器：一次定义生成可复用的轻量级工件，后续调用廉价且可离线运行。

Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Adaptive Companionship for Group-Following Robots: Handling Dynamically Changing Group Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01287)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的社会机器人自适应群体陪伴方法，通过VLM的语义推理能力推断陪伴位置、维持社交距离和理解群体动态，并结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器确保稳定性和安全性。实验表明，该方法在成功率上提升15%，碰撞率降低25%，用户研究也表明陪伴行为自然且符合社交规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类群体通常不遵循固定队形，使得机器人在维持自然陪伴行为时面临巨大挑战，需要开发能够适应群体动态的陪伴方法。

Method: 首先检测群体成员，感知模块生成交互群体空间的视觉表示作为VLM输入，结合MPPI控制器进行路径规划，实现自适应群体陪伴。

Result: 在五个场景中，该方法相比基线方法成功率提升15%，碰撞率降低25%；用户研究显示陪伴行为被视为自然且社交恰当的。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的群体陪伴方法能有效提升机器人在非固定队形群体中陪伴的自然性和安全性，具备良好的社交适应性。

Abstract: Accompanying a group of humans is an essential aspect of developing human-like social cognition in robots. However, human groups typically do not follow fixed formations, which poses significant challenges for robots in maintaining natural companionship behaviors. In this paper, we propose an adaptive group-accompaniment method for social robots based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), leveraging their semantic reasoning capabilities to infer companion positions, maintain social distances, and understand group dynamics. The members of the group are first detected, and a perceptual module generates visual representations of the interaction group space as input to the VLM, which is then combined with a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller to ensure stability and safety. Experimental evaluations across five scenarios show that the proposed method enables robots to accompany the group effectively, demonstrating a 15\% improvement in success rate and a 25\% reduction in collision rate compared to baseline approaches. Additionally, a user study indicates that the generated companionship behaviors are perceived as natural and socially appropriate.

</details>


### [186] [WaveLander: A Generalizable Hierarchical Control Framework for UAV Landing on Wave-Disturbed Platforms via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01281)
*Chun-Kit Li,Iok Long Sit,Ming Fung Siu,Ka Yu Kui,Hin Wang Lin,Pengyu Wang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WaveLander，一种基于强化学习的分层控制框架，将无人机在波浪扰动海上平台上的自主着陆问题解耦为高层RL垂直速度决策与低层飞行稳定控制，在仿真中展现出鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在波浪扰动海洋平台上的自主着陆因平台随机运动、姿态时变和触地条件不确定而极具挑战，现有基于模型的方法需要精确运动预测和在线优化，端到端学习方法则训练复杂度高且可解释性差。

Method: 提出WaveLander分层控制框架：上层RL策略将平台相对观测映射为标量垂直速度参考指令，下层传统飞行控制器维持姿态稳定和横向跟踪，将动态平台着陆问题降维为低维、时序感知的控制问题。

Result: 在随机波浪诱导平台运动仿真下，WaveLander实现了鲁棒的着陆性能，并能泛化到未见过的扰动条件。

Conclusion: 分层学习控制方法在海上无人机回收中具有潜力，通过将强化学习与传统控制结合，可在降低训练复杂度的同时实现鲁棒着陆行为。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) on wave-disturbed marine platforms remains challenging due to stochastic platform motion, time-varying platform attitude, and uncertain touchdown conditions. Existing model-based methods often require accurate motion prediction and online optimization, while end-to-end learning approaches may suffer from high training complexity and limited interpretability. This paper presents WaveLander, a hierarchical control framework via reinforcement learning (RL) that decouples vertical landing decision-making from low-level flight stabilization. The RL policy maps a compact platform-relative observation to a scalar vertical velocity reference, while a conventional low-level flight controller maintains attitude stability and lateral tracking. This formulation reduces dynamic platform landing to a low-dimensional, timing-aware control problem and enables smooth landing behavior without explicit switching rules. Simulation results under randomized wave-induced platform motions show that WaveLander achieves robust landing performance and generalizes to unseen disturbance conditions, demonstrating the potential of hierarchical learning-based control for marine UAV recovery.

</details>


### [187] [Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models via Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01378)
*William English,Hao Zheng,Rickard Ewetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号安全引导机制，用于基于流匹配的VLA模型，通过在去噪过程中修正轨迹实现预测性碰撞避免，在SafeLIBERO基准上实现82.8%碰撞避免率和81.6%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的安全措施仅能防止机器人下一步动作引起的碰撞，缺乏前瞻性的碰撞避免能力。

Method: 将安全强制执行形式化为最小范数约束优化问题，在迭代神经流匹配的去噪过程中修正中间轨迹预测的安全违规，通过符号约束满足与神经轨迹生成的交错实现预测性碰撞避免。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO基准上达到82.8%碰撞避免和81.6%任务成功，相比单步方法分别提升6.3%和19.8%，在长时域任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: 该神经符号安全引导机制能够有效实现预测性碰撞避免，特别是在长时域任务中表现优异，为VLA模型的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their real-world deployment remains limited by the lack of effective safety measures. Specifically, existing safety measures only prevent collisions caused by the robot's next action. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic safety guidance mechanism for flow matching based VLAs that enables predictive collision avoidance. Flow matching based VLAs determine the next actions by predicting a trajectory (a sequence of actions) through an iterative neural flow matching process. Our method formulates safety enforcement as a minimum-norm constrained optimization problem that corrects safety violations during the denoising process of noisy intermediate trajectory predictions. By analyzing predicted trajectories and applying corrections during iterative denoising, our approach anticipates collisions before they become unavoidable. This interleaving of symbolic constraint satisfaction with neural trajectory generation enables predictive collision avoidance rather than reactive intervention. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, our method achieves 82.8% collision avoidance and 81.6% task success, a 6.3% and 19.8% improvement respectively over single-step methods, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks where compounding distribution shift is most pronounced. Video demonstrations of our approach are included on our project page at https://willenglish.tech/SafetyGuidedFlowMatching/.

</details>


### [188] [The Three Dimensions of ROS 2 Middleware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01304)
*Sanghoon Lee,Taehun Kim,Angelo Corsaro,Kyung-Joon Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统调查了ROS 2中间件（DDS和Zenoh）在动态无线环境下的结构局限性，从空间、时间、状态三个维度分析了分布式机器人中间件的架构权衡，并提出了未来研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2中间件在动态和资源受限的无线环境中存在结构性限制，但缺乏对这些限制的系统性分析和形式化描述，亟需一个统一的框架来理解其架构权衡。

Method: 采用系统性调查方法，提出一个概念框架，从空间（Space）、时间（Time）、状态（State）三个结构维度分析中间件架构，并通过回顾现有实现和先前研究来组织中间件研究。

Result: 识别出三个关键结构权衡：空间抽象会掩盖网络可变性并削弱时间保证；状态连续性机制引入计算和网络开销，与时间关键通信竞争；这些权衡表征了当代机器人中间件的实际局限性。

Conclusion: 通过综合架构模式并识别当前建模和分析方法的空白，为鲁棒且可扩展的机器人中间件架构提出了一个原则性的研究路线图。

Abstract: ROS 2 (Robot Operating System 2) has emerged as the de facto standard for modern robot software development, with middleware implementations such as the Data Distribution Service (DDS) and Zenoh forming the core infrastructure for distributed robotic communication. Despite their architectural flexibility, these middleware systems exhibit structural limitations, particularly under dynamic and resource-constrained wireless environments. This paper presents a systematic survey of ROS 2 middleware and introduces a conceptual framework to examine its architectural limits through three structural dimensions required by distributed robotic systems, namely Space, Time, and State. We first provide a structured analysis of middleware architecture and operational dynamics, including discovery, data exchange, and state management mechanisms. Building on this foundation, we formalize Time as temporal predictability for control loops, Space as spatial abstraction from physical topology to enable modular deployment, and State as contextual continuity despite dynamic node participation and intermittent connectivity. Through a comprehensive review of existing implementations and prior studies, we organize middleware research according to the structural trade-offs that arise among these dimensions. Under constrained wireless conditions, spatial abstraction can obscure network variability and weaken temporal guarantees, while mechanisms that preserve state continuity introduce computational and network overhead that competes with time-critical communication. These interactions reveal structural trade-offs that characterize the practical limits of contemporary robot middleware. By synthesizing architectural patterns and identifying gaps in current modeling and analysis approaches, this survey outlines a principled research roadmap for robust and scalable robotic middleware architectures.

</details>


### [189] [CommonRoad-Game: A Human-in-the-Loop Simulation Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01382)
*Yunfei Bi,Youran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CommonRoad-Game，一个轻量级的人机协同仿真框架，紧密集成于CommonRoad平台，通过多线程架构和鲁棒同步机制实现自动驾驶规划器与人类驾驶员的实时交互，支持可复现的场景生成和多智能体仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动规划仿真平台存在三大局限：依赖录播数据集、缺乏实时人机交互接口、与自动驾驶生态集成度低；同时许多人在环仿真器计算开销大，不适合早期自动驾驶研究的快速原型开发。

Method: 提出多线程架构与鲁棒同步机制，将仿真时间与墙钟时间对齐，实现自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶员之间确定性的时序一致交互；提供场景生成模块记录驾驶日志，支持从人在环实验中构建多样化、可复现的测试场景。

Result: 实验表明CommonRoad-Game实现了稳定的时间同步，支持可扩展的多智能体仿真，并能无缝集成CommonRoad兼容的运动规划器以生成交互式驾驶场景。

Conclusion: CommonRoad-Game作为一个轻量级人在环仿真框架，有效填补了现有平台在实时人类交互、生态集成和计算效率方面的空白，为自动驾驶早期研究提供了灵活高效的测试工具。

Abstract: Motion planning algorithms should be evaluated in human-in-the-loop environments to ensure they produce safe and efficient behaviors during interactions. However, existing simulation platforms often rely on recorded datasets, lack dedicated interfaces for real-time human interaction, or remain weakly integrated with an autonomous driving ecosystem. Moreover, many human-in-the-loop simulators are computationally intensive by design, making them less suitable for rapid prototyping and flexible experimentation in early-stage autonomous driving research. To address these limitations, we present CommonRoad-Game, a lightweight human-in-the-loop simulation framework tightly integrated with the CommonRoad platform, focusing on the systematic testing of motion planners with human participation and the analysis of human driving behaviors in interactive scenarios. We introduce a multi-threaded architecture with a robust synchronization mechanism that aligns simulation time with wall-clock time, enabling deterministic and temporally consistent interaction between autonomous and human-driven vehicles. In addition, the framework provides a scenario generation module that records driving logs, allowing diverse and reproducible test cases to be constructed from human-in-the-loop experiments. Experimental results demonstrate that CommonRoad-Game achieves stable temporal synchronization, supports scalable multi-agent simulation, and seamlessly integrates CommonRoad-compatible motion planners to generate interactive driving scenarios. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Yunfei-Bi8/CommonRoad-Game.

</details>


### [190] [BIFROST: Bridging Invariant Feature Representation for Observation-space Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01410)
*Yunfu Deng,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIFROST通过跨域双模拟目标学习共享历史编码器，将产生等效长期行为的观测-动作序列映射到相近的潜在状态，从而在仿真和现实之间建立共享结构，实现零样本迁移的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的sim2real方法通常通过独立的适应模块分别处理视觉或动力学域间隙，当两种间隙共存时再进行组合或分层处理。然而，sim2real的根本前提是仿真与现实任务之间存在共享结构——等效配置下执行等效动作会产生等效长期结果。因此，本文旨在探索如何从原始观测中识别并利用这种共享结构，训练出能实现零样本迁移的策略。

Method: 提出BIFROST框架，在配对的跨域数据上通过跨域双模拟目标（cross-domain bisimulation objective）学习共享历史编码器。该目标将产生等效长期行为的观测-动作序列映射到相近的潜在状态，忽略域特定的渲染或物理差异。策略在仿真环境中的这些潜在状态上进行训练，可直接零样本迁移到现实环境。

Result: 在sim2sim视觉导航、sim2real接触式操作任务以及视觉伺服任务上，BIFROST均实现了有效的迁移。在同时存在视觉和动力学域间隙的情况下，BIFROST优于域适应和协同训练基线方法。

Conclusion: BIFROST通过跨域双模拟学习共享潜在表示，有效解决了仿真与现实中同时存在视觉和动力学差异时的sim2real零样本迁移问题。

Abstract: Sim2real transfer for robot policy learning suffers due to mismatch between simulation and reality. Existing methods typically address each gap in isolation through separate adaptation modules, which are composed or layered when both gaps coexist. Yet the basis for attempting sim2real in the first place is that there is shared structure between a task in simulation and reality, where equivalent actions from equivalent configurations produce equivalent long term outcomes regardless of domain specific differences in rendering or physics. In this paper, we study whether we can identify and exploit this shared structure from raw observations to train a policy that enables zero shot transfer. We introduce BIFROST, which learns a shared history encoder on paired cross-domain data via cross-domain bisimulation objective: observation-action sequences leading to equivalent long-term behavior are mapped to nearby latent states, regardless of domain. Policies trained on these latent states in simulation transfer zero-shot to reality. We provide empirical evidence on sim2sim visual navigation and sim2real contact rich manipulation task and visual servoing task that BIFROST achieves effective transfer where domain adaptation and co-training baselines fail under both visual and dynamics domain gaps.

</details>


### [191] [SE(2) Navigation Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01454)
*Shuyang Shi,Kaixian Qu,Changan Chen,Ines Kast,Yuntao Ma,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SE(2)导航网格，一种编码偏航相关可通行性的多边形表示方法，并开发了A*-String Pulling-A*分层路径规划策略，支持复杂多层级环境中非圆形机器人的高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航网格假设偏航不变的可通行性，不适用于非圆形机器人在受限空间中的导航；点云和体素占用地图缺乏显式表面结构，而稠密三角网格上的直接路径规划计算开销过大。

Method: 使用足迹掩码评估偏航相关的可通行性，构建具有显式平移和旋转连接的多层偏航图；提出A*-String Pulling-A*分层路径规划策略，分层优化位置与朝向；支持从流式点云在线增量更新SE(2)导航网格。

Result: 仿真中SE(2) NavMesh比经典NavMesh多捕获50%以上的可通行区域；SE(2) NavMesh + ASA管线在受限环境中持续优于基于采样的基线；真实机器人实验验证了在线实时生成与成功导航。

Conclusion: SE(2)导航网格有效解决了非圆形机器人在复杂多层级环境中的导航问题，兼顾可通行性表示的精度与路径规划的计算效率，并支持在线更新。

Abstract: Global navigation for ground robots in complex multi-level environments requires representations that accurately capture traversable regions while enabling efficient path planning. Current approaches present key limitations: Point clouds and volumetric occupancy maps lack explicit surface structure for traversability estimation, whereas direct pathfinding on dense triangle meshes is computationally prohibitive. Navigation meshes mitigate these challenges through polygonal abstraction of the underlying mesh, but assume yaw-invariant traversability, rendering them unsuitable for non-circular robots in constrained spaces. We propose SE(2) Navigation Mesh (SE(2) NavMesh), a polygonal representation of traversable regions that encodes yaw-dependent traversability. Our method evaluates traversability using footprint masks and constructs a graph over yaw-specific layers with explicit translational and rotational connectivity. Grounded in this representation, we develop an A*-String Pulling-A* (ASA) pathfinding strategy that hierarchically optimizes robot position and heading. We also present an online method that incrementally updates the SE(2) NavMesh from streaming point clouds during concurrent geometry reconstruction. In simulation, the SE(2) NavMesh captures over 50% more traversable area than classical NavMeshes, and the SE(2) NavMesh + ASA pipeline consistently outperforms sampling-based baselines in constrained environments. Extensive real-world experiments on a physical robot validate real-time online generation and successful navigation across multiple environments.

</details>


### [192] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型平台，通过万向节将圆柱体安装在四旋翼上方形成飞行倒立摆，采用自适应反步控制和动态面姿态控制实现推力矢量控制，在仿真和室内运动捕捉实验中验证了精确轨迹跟踪和扰动补偿能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为验证运载火箭类系统的推力矢量控制策略，提供一种低成本、低风险的测试平台。

Method: 将耦合系统建模为单个轴对称刚体；采用二维球面上的降阶姿态表示以利用轴对称性并解耦偏航与推力矢量方向；设计自适应反步控制器实现存在未知常值扰动时的几乎全局轨迹跟踪；通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为；将四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态面的姿态控制器跟踪期望推力矢量。

Result: 仿真和室内运动捕捉实验验证了精确的轨迹跟踪能力和有效的扰动补偿效果。

Conclusion: QuadRocket可作为推力矢量控制机器人车辆的多功能测试平台。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [193] [A Reconfigurable Rocker-Bogie Robot for High Step Climbing and Turning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01554)
*Kento Koizumi,Tomoaki Ohba,Yuta Saito,Takeya Morito,Kenji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构摇臂-转向架机构，通过主动摆动转向架在四轮和六轮构型间切换，在保持高越障能力的同时实现零半径转弯，且转弯速度是传统机构的5倍以上，所需扭矩仅为传统机构的17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摇臂-转向架机构虽具有优越的越障能力，但转弯效率低、所需扭矩大，难以在狭窄空间内灵活机动。本研究旨在设计一种兼具高越障能力和高效转弯能力的可重构机构。

Method: 在转向架关节处安装电机实现主动摆动，使系统可在四轮与六轮构型间切换；在摇臂后端安装全向轮，四轮构型下基于差速驱动模型实现平滑转弯；通过原型样机进行实验验证。

Result: 所提机构实现零半径转弯，转弯速度是传统六轮不可转向抓地轮摇臂-转向架机构的5倍以上，总平均轮扭矩仅为传统机构的17%；成功攀爬40cm台阶，平均攀爬时间6.4s。

Conclusion: 可重构摇臂-转向架机构有效兼顾了高效转弯与高越障能力，在需要狭窄空间机动和越障的应用场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: This study proposes a reconfigurable rocker-bogie mechanism that achieves efficient turning motion with a small number of actuators while maintaining high step-climbing capability. By installing motors at the bogie joints and actively swinging up and down bogies, the system enables switching between four-wheel and six-wheel configurations. Omnidirectional wheels are mounted on the rear ends of the rockers, allowing smooth turning in the four-wheel configuration based on a differential-drive model. Experimental evaluation using a prototype robot demonstrated that the proposed mechanism achieves zero-radius turning at a speed more than five times that of a conventional rocker-bogie mechanism equipped with six non-steerable grip wheels, while requiring only approximately 17% of the total average wheel torque. In addition, the robot successfully climbed a 40 cm step with an average climbing time of 6.4 s, confirming its high turning and step-climbing performance.

</details>


### [194] [Multi-Rate Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Wall-Supported Bipedal Locomotion of Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01574)
*Taizoon Chunawala,Jeeseop Kim,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多速率非线性模型预测控制（MR-NMPC）的分层规划与控制框架，使四足机器人在受限环境中借助墙壁辅助实现混合双足运动，并通过足部落点规划的优化显著提升了动态稳定性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在受限环境中执行混合双足运动时，控制器需要同时规划接触点和连续质心/姿态轨迹，面临非线性动力学、单边接触约束、欠驱动及动力学切换等挑战，传统启发式足部规划方法动态稳定性不足。

Method: 高层采用MR-NMPC基于单刚体动力学模型同时规划离散接触点轨迹和连续质心/姿态轨迹；低层采用基于虚拟约束和二次规划的非线性全身控制器来执行全阶动力学并跟踪高层参考轨迹。

Result: 在Unitree A1四足机器人上的数值仿真表明，所提方法在粗糙地形和外部扰动下实现了稳健的墙壁辅助双足运动；相比基于启发式足部规划的常规MPC，在不规则地形高速运动中成功率提升了2.9倍。

Conclusion: 将接触点规划集成到多速率最优控制框架中有效提升了动态稳定性，验证了MR-NMPC框架在处理受限环境中混合双足运动任务方面的优势。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel layered planning and control framework based on multi-rate nonlinear model predictive control (MR-NMPC) that enables quadrupedal robots to perform hybrid bipedal locomotion with wall-assisted support in constrained environments. Real-time trajectory optimization for this locomotion presents significant challenges, as the controller must simultaneously plan for both the contact points and the continuous trajectories of the robot's center of mass (CoM) and orientation within the robot's nonlinear dynamics while accounting for unilateral contact constraints, underactuation, and the switching nature of the robot's dynamics. At the high level of the control framework, an MR-NMPC is proposed, which dynamically plans both the discrete-time trajectories of the contact points and the continuous-time trajectories of the CoM and orientation, using a single rigid body (SRB) dynamics model. By incorporating contact-point planning within the multi-rate optimal control framework, this approach enhances dynamic stability compared to heuristic foot placement strategies. At the low level of the control framework, a nonlinear whole-body controller (WBC) based on virtual constraints and a quadratic program enforces full-order dynamics and tracks the MR-NMPC references. The proposed approach is validated through extensive numerical simulations demonstrating the robust wall-assisted bipedal locomotion of a Unitree A1 quadrupedal robot on rough terrains and under external disturbances in a constrained environment. Comparative analysis shows that the proposed MR-NMPC achieves a 2.9 times higher success rate compared to conventional MPC with heuristic-based foot placement strategies in negotiating irregular terrain at high speeds.

</details>


### [195] [Path planning for unmanned naval surface vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01631)
*Daniel G. Schwartz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合全局路径规划器（融合Grassfire、改进Grassfire及新型概率路线图）与局部路径规划器（基于障碍物相对运动方向的高层决策逻辑，引导USV从障碍物后方绕行）的无人水面艇避障新方法，仿真验证了其有效性并与D*算法进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人水面艇（USV）在固定障碍物实时规避方面已有诸多方法，但针对移动障碍物（如船只、游泳者等）的规避仍存在挑战。本文旨在同时解决固定与移动障碍物的避障问题。

Method: 全局路径规划器融合三种算法：经典Grassfire、改进版Grassfire以及一种新型更直观的概率路线图（PRM）变体；局部路径规划器基于障碍物相对于USV全局路径的运动方向，采用高层决策逻辑，系统性地引导USV从障碍物后方绕行，而非平行行驶等待穿越机会。

Result: 仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并与经典的D*算法及其他动态路径规划系统进行了对比，结果表明该方法在引导USV从移动障碍物后方绕行方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 该组合方法能有效同时处理固定与移动障碍物的规避问题，局部规划器通过"后方绕行"策略优于不强制从障碍物后方通过的D*等算法。

Abstract: There nowadays is a myriad of approaches to real-time avoidance of fixed obstacles for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) and, to a lesser extent, also the task of avoiding moving obstacles such as boats, ships, swimmers, and other USVs, but both topics still present challenges. This paper offers novel approaches to both of these problems. It uses a combination of a global path planner, which finds a path from a start point to a goal point that avoids fixed obstacles (given that their locations are known in advance), and a local path planner, which can circumnavigate a moving obstacle (as well as any previously unknown fixed obstacles). The global planner is novel in that it employs a combination of three path planners, one known in the literature as Grassfire, one that is a new modification of Grassfire, and one that is a new, and arguably more intuitive, version of the well-known Probabilistic Roadmap. The local planner is novel in that it employs a higher-level decision logic based on its observations regarding the direction of movement of the obstacle relative to the USVs global path. This logic enables the USV to determine the best strategy for avoiding the obstacle by systematically routing the vehicle behind the obstacle rather than running parallel to it until the opportunity to pass appears. Simulations are provided that validate these claims. For comparison with other systems, the simulations include an implementation of the well-known D* algorithm, and the discussion covers additional dynamic path planning systems, which, like D*, do not necessarily route the vehicle behind the moving obstacle.

</details>


### [196] [One Demonstration Is Enough for Real-World Robotic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01651)
*Yuwan Liu,Hongze Yu,Song Liu,Yuhan Wang,Junge Zhang,Yaodong Yang,Yuanpei Chen,Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoSERL是一个利用单次人类演示自动干预真实世界机器人强化学习的框架，通过滑动窗口干预、安全恢复和自动终止机制，在6个接触密集型任务上优于使用20次演示的SERL等方法，达到100%插入任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理硬件上学习机器人控制策略面临数据收集成本高和奖励设计困难的问题。现有结合演示和强化学习的方法要么需要大量演示数据，要么在训练过程中依赖持续的人工干预，限制了实际部署的可行性。

Method: 提出AutoSERL框架，包含三个互补机制：(1) 滑动窗口干预机制，持续引导探索以避免局部最优和不安全偏离；(2) 安全恢复机制，通过预定义轨迹恢复点检测和纠正失败状态；(3) 干预终止准则，当策略能独立完成任务时自动关闭引导以保留探索优势。

Result: 在两个机器人平台的6个接触密集型操控任务（插入、挂放、铰链类任务）上，AutoSERL始终优于以20次演示初始化的SERL、行为克隆及单次模仿学习基线MILES，达到与人类循环干预的HIL-SERL相当的性能，插入任务成功率达100%，对位置变化具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AutoSERL表明仅需单次人类演示即可实现高效的现实世界机器人强化学习，通过自动化干预过程克服了数据效率和人工介入的瓶颈，为实际机器人部署提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning effective robot control policies on physical hardware is challenging due to costly data collection and the difficulty of reward specification. Prior work has incorporated demonstrations into reinforcement learning (RL), yet existing approaches either require large numbers of demonstrations or depend on continuous human intervention during training. To address these limitations, we present AutoSERL, a framework that leverages a single demonstration to fully automate the intervention process in real-world robot RL. The framework includes three complementary mechanisms to accomplish certain tasks: a sliding window intervention mechanism that continuously guides exploration to prevent local optima and unsafe deviations, a safety recovery mechanism that detects and corrects failure states via predefined trajectory recovery points, and an intervention termination criterion that automatically disables guidance once the policy can independently complete the task, preserving its exploration advantage. We evaluate AutoSERL on six contact-intensive manipulation tasks across two robot platforms, spanning insertion, hanging, and hinge-based tasks. AutoSERL consistently outperforms SERL initialized with 20 demonstrations, behavior cloning, and MILES -- a dedicated one-shot imitation learning baseline -- across all tasks while matching HIL-SERL, achieves 100% success rate on insertion tasks, and demonstrates improved robustness to positional variations, all from a single demonstration. Code and videos are available on our project website: https://autoserl.github.io/.

</details>


### [197] [Imagining the Sense of Touch: Touch-Informed Manipulation via Imagined Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01684)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Jyun-Chi Hu,Yosuke Saka,Quan Khanh Luu,Jiuzhou Lei,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Bihao Zhang,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacImag 框架，从视觉和本体感受预测触觉信号以指导操作策略，在仿真和真实实验中无需触觉传感器即可提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感在机器人操作中潜力巨大，但实际部署受限于硬件的脆弱性、标定需求和维护负担，因此探索是否可以在部署时无需触觉传感器的情况下受益于触觉知识。

Method: 提出 TacImag（触觉想象框架），从配对视觉-触觉示范中训练，在测试时仅用视觉观测预测触觉信号（触觉力场或触觉图像），并将生成的信号用于引导操作策略。

Result: 在6个仿真和4个真实操作任务中，想象触觉信号一致提升操作性能；真实实验中，想象力场使接触敏感任务平均提升44.4%，想象触觉图像使纹理敏感任务平均提升23.3%。

Conclusion: 触觉想象的有效性强烈依赖于触觉表征与任务需求的关系；触觉想象并非简单地恢复缺失的触觉测量，而是作为一种接触感知的监督形式，将微妙的视觉交互线索转化为操作策略更易利用的表征。

Abstract: Tactile sensing can substantially improve contact-rich robotic manipulation, yet its practical deployment remains limited by the fragility, calibration requirements, and maintenance burden of tactile hardware. This raises a fundamental question: can robots benefit from tactile knowledge without requiring tactile sensors at deployment? We present TacImag, a tactile imagination framework that predicts tactile observations from vision and proprioception and uses the generated signals to guide manipulation policies. Trained from paired visuotactile demonstrations, TacImag enables touch-informed manipulation using only visual observations at test time. We evaluate TacImag on six simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks. Across simulation and real-world experiments, imagined tactile observations consistently improve manipulation performance without requiring tactile hardware. In real-world experiments, imagined force fields improve contact-sensitive tasks by 44.4% on average, whereas imagined tactile images improve texture-sensitive tasks by 23.3%, revealing that the effectiveness of tactile imagination depends strongly on the relationship between tactile representation and task requirements. Our results further suggest that tactile imagination does not simply recover missing tactile measurements. Instead, it acts as a form of contact-aware supervision that transforms subtle visual interaction cues into representations that are easier for manipulation policies to exploit.

</details>


### [198] [CoRe: Combined Rewards with Vision-Language Model Feedback for Preference-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01721)
*Hexian Ni,Tao Lu,Yinghao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRe是一个混合奖励框架，将奖励分解为基于任务知识的正式奖励（FR）和从观察中学习的残差奖励（RR），结合视觉语言模型反馈实现无需人工参与的偏好对齐策略学习，在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操作任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计奖励困难且可能导致次优策略，而基于偏好的学习奖励存在效率低下和训练不稳定的问题。受认知科学中人类学习的双重性质启发，希望将两种奖励方式的优势结合。

Method: 提出CoRe框架：(1) 正式奖励模块（FRM）利用VLM基于任务知识和偏好反馈迭代设计与优化正式奖励；(2) 残差奖励模块（RRM）利用VLM生成偏好标签，从视频级偏好中学习残差奖励，弥补正式奖励的不足。

Result: 在10个仿真机器人操作任务和5个真实世界任务上，CoRe在策略学习效果和效率方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过正式奖励与残差奖励的协同作用，CoRe能够自动构建可靠、高效且与偏好对齐的奖励函数，无需人类参与。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Hand-crafted rewards are often difficult to specify and may lead to suboptimal policies, while learned rewards from preferences can suffer from inefficiency and unstable training. Inspired by the dual nature of human learning explored in cognitive science, we decompose rewards into two complementary components: Formal Rewards (FR), explicitly designed based on task knowledge, and Residual Rewards (RR), learned from observations to capture implicit and nuanced preferences. Based on this decomposition, we propose CoRe, a hybrid framework that integrates FR and RR with vision-language models (VLMs) feedback to achieve preference-aligned policies without human involvement. Our contributions are twofold: (1) We propose a Formal Reward Module (FRM) that leverages VLMs to iteratively design and optimize FR based on task knowledge and preference feedback, enabling the continual improvement of policy during training; (2) We introduce a Residual Reward Module (RRM) that learns RR from video-level preference by employing VLMs to generate preference labels and capturing nuanced rewards that complement FR, ensuring alignment with human intent. Through the synergy of FRM and RRM, CoRe enables the automatic construction of reliable rewards that are efficient and preference-aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoRe outperforms existing approaches in terms of policy learning effectiveness and efficiency on ten robotic manipulation tasks in simulation and five real-worlds. Videos can be found on our project website: https://core-2026.github.io/

</details>


### [199] [Lightweight Safe Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01794)
*Shenghui Zhang,YuXuan Gao,Songwei Zhao,Jifeng Hu,Zijing Zhang,Hechang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种安全约束下的感知-控制一体化无人机导航框架，使用轻量级网络编码稀疏观测，结合基于Lagrangian的安全PPO算法和课程学习，在密集障碍物和高速飞行场景下实现了更高的成功率、安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习无人机导航方法在稀疏感知和动态约束下缺乏显式安全机制，导致不安全探索和危险行为；安全RL方法通过投影策略到安全动作集可能引入不稳定性；多数方法依赖密集输入或大网络，计算负担重，难以轻量化部署。

Method: 1) 轻量级网络：采用非对称和深度可分离卷积编码稀疏观测为碰撞风险感知特征；2) 约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）建模：在分层控制架构下将任务形式化为CMDP；3) Lagrangian安全PPO算法求解；4) 课程学习提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在不同障碍物密度和飞行速度的实验中，该方法相比现有强化学习基线实现了更高的成功率、更好的安全性和更高的效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全约束感知-控制一体化框架有效解决了无人机在密集环境中的自主导航难题，兼顾了安全性、效率和轻量化部署需求。

Abstract: With the rapid development of autonomous aerial systems, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed in applications such as inspection, environmental monitoring, and rescue, creating growing demand for reliable autonomous navigation. However, autonomous UAV navigation in dense environments remains challenging under sparse perception and dynamic constraints. Most reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack explicit safety mechanisms, leading to unsafe exploration, unstable training, and risky behaviors, especially during high-speed flight. Even in safe RL approaches, safety is often enforced by projecting policy outputs onto a safe action set, which may introduce instability. Meanwhile, many learning-based methods rely on dense inputs or large networks, increasing computational burden and limiting lightweight onboard deployment. Facing the above challenges, we propose a safety-constrained perception-control integrated framework for UAV navigation. A lightweight network encodes sparse observations into collision-risk-aware features using asymmetric and depthwise separable convolutions. We formulate the task as a constrained Markov decision process within a hierarchical control architecture and solve it using a Lagrangian-based safe PPO algorithm. Curriculum learning further improves training stability. Experiments with varying obstacle densities and flight speeds demonstrate higher success rates, improved safety, and better efficiency than existing reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [200] [VLA-Corrector: Lightweight Detect-and-Correct Inference for Adaptive Action Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01804)
*Yi Pan,Miao Pan,Qi Lu,Jiaming Huang,Man Zhang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Jie Zhang,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-Corrector，一种轻量级校正推理框架，通过潜在空间视觉监测器（LVM）在线检测视觉动态偏差并触发截断与在线梯度引导（OGG）重规划，将固定动作块策略转变为事件触发的自适应动作视界，在不重训骨干网络的前提下提升VLA策略在接触密集任务中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略采用固定动作块（action chunk）的"预测-盲执行"模式，在接触密集的物理交互中，局部微小扰动会在开环盲区内快速放大，导致复合误差和任务失败，缺乏闭环反应能力。

Method: 提出VLA-Corrector框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM)——在潜在空间中持续比较预测视觉特征与实际视觉特征的演化，在线检测动态偏差；（2）Online Gradient Guidance (OGG)——检测到持续偏差时触发截断事件，丢弃剩余过时动作并调用校正重规划。该机制将固定动作视界转变为事件触发的自适应动作视界。

Result: 在无需重新训练VLA骨干网络的前提下，VLA-Corrector能够中断复合误差的累积，同时保留动作块机制的大部分效率优势，显著提升长视界、接触密集的机器人操作任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLA-Corrector通过轻量级的检测-校正机制，有效缓解了静态动作视界下执行鲁棒性与策略调用频率之间的权衡，为开放式动作块策略注入了闭环响应能力，是一种即插即用的通用校正框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently achieved strong progress in embodied intelligence. To reduce policy-call frequency while preserving temporal coherence, most generative policies adopt an action chunk mechanism, executing multiple future actions in an open-loop manner under a fixed action horizon. However, this "predict-then-blindly-execute" paradigm sacrifices closed-loop reactivity: in contact-rich physical interactions, even small local perturbations can rapidly amplify within the open-loop blind spot, leading to compounding errors and ultimately task failure. To address this limitation, we propose VLA-Corrector, a lightweight corrective inference framework for action-chunked VLA policies. Without modifying the backbone policy weights, VLA-Corrector introduces a lightweight Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM) that continuously compares predicted and actual visual feature evolution, enabling online detection of visual dynamics deviations. Once persistent deviation is detected, the system triggers a truncation event, discards the remaining stale actions, and invokes corrective replanning via Online Gradient Guidance (OGG). The detect-and-correct mechanism of VLA-Corrector naturally induces an event-triggered adaptive action horizon: it preserves long-horizon execution when the current chunk remains reliable, and invokes short-horizon corrective replanning when execution begins to drift. In doing so, VLA-Corrector mitigates the trade-off imposed by static horizons between execution robustness and policy-call frequency. It can be integrated into different VLA models without further retraining the VLA backbone, interrupting compounding errors while preserving much of the efficiency benefit of action chunking and substantially improving robustness in long-horizon, contact-rich robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [201] [DL-SLAM: Enabling High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting SLAM in Dynamic Environments based on Dual-Level Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01860)
*Ziheng Xu,Qingfeng Li,Xuefeng Liu,Chen Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DL-SLAM提出了一种基于双层级概率框架的单目高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过结合语义和几何信息计算动态概率图，实现无伪影的静态地图构建，跟踪精度提升最高达13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态SLAM方法要么直接丢弃预定义的动态物体，忽略了瞬态静态物体对位姿估计的几何约束价值；要么采用逐像素不确定度图来利用瞬态静态物体，但会错误地将它们集成到静态地图中产生伪影，且纯几何方法在不确定度图中导致模糊的物体边界。

Method: 提出双层级概率框架：1) 结合语义和几何信息计算像素级动态概率图；2) 将像素级概率提升到3D并聚合得到每个实例的物体级动态概率；3) 利用物体级概率对动态高斯体进行分类剔除，生成无伪影静态地图；4) 静态地图提供几何一致性引导，反向优化像素级概率。

Result: DL-SLAM在跟踪精度上比现有方法提升高达13%，同时生成高保真语义地图。

Conclusion: 双层级概率框架有效解决了瞬态静态物体的利用与伪影问题，验证了语义与几何信息融合在动态SLAM中的优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in dense dynamic Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). Prevailing methods typically discard predefined dynamic objects, ignoring that transiently static objects offer valuable geometric constraints for pose estimation. A recent work attempts to leverage this potential by employing per-pixel uncertainty maps to quantify the magnitude of motion. While this approach enables transiently static objects to enhance pose estimation, it erroneously integrates these objects into the static map, resulting in persistent artifacts. Moreover, its reliance on purely geometric information leads to ambiguous object boundaries in the uncertainty maps. To overcome these limitations, we present DL-SLAM, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system built upon a novel dual-level probabilistic framework. Our method computes dynamic probability maps by combining semantic and geometric information. These pixel-level probabilities are lifted to 3D and aggregated to derive an object-level dynamic probability for each instance. Object-level probability enables the categorical pruning of dynamic Gaussians, resulting in an artifact-free static map. The static map, in turn, provides a geometrically consistent guidance to refine the pixel-wise probabilities, enhancing their reliability. Experimental results demonstrate that DL-SLAM outperforms existing approaches, improving tracking accuracy by up to 13\% while generating high-fidelity semantic maps.

</details>


### [202] [SPLC: Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01925)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Fu,Haiwen Hu,Shiquan Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLC算法，通过社会偏好反馈机制自动生成偏好数据，消除离线强化学习中众机器人导航任务的奖励函数设计需求，显著提升了社交合规行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人运动的固有复杂性使得设计有效的奖励函数来促进社交合规的机器人行为一直是一个持续的挑战，现有方法需要精细的奖励设计。

Method: 提出Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC)算法，核心创新是引入社会偏好反馈机制，通过原则性的偏好评估标准自动生成偏好数据，显式考虑行人动力学复杂性以缓解奖励偏差并系统量化广泛的社会规范。

Result: 与离线RL方法集成的SPLC在标准性能指标上持续优于最先进的基线方法；在TurtleBot4上的真实世界实验进一步验证了其在人机共存场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPLC通过社会偏好学习有效消除了对详细奖励设计的依赖，促进了社交合规的机器人导航行为。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant potential for crowd robot navigation in human-robot coexistence applications. However, the inherent complexity of pedestrian motion renders the design of effective reward functions for promoting socially compliant robot behaviors a persistent challenge. This paper proposes a Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC) algorithm to eliminate the need for detailed reward design. Its core innovation lies in the introduction of a social preference feedback mechanism to automatically generate preference data through principled preference evaluation criteria. By explicitly accounting for the intricacies of pedestrian dynamics, the pipeline mitigates the reward bias and facilitates the systematic quantification of broad social norms, thereby fostering socially compliant behaviors. Extensive experiments integrating SPLC with offline RL methods demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across standard performance metrics. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the TurtleBot4 further validate the effectiveness of SPLC in practical human-robot coexistence settings. Our code and video demos are available at https://github.com/sklus949/SPLC.

</details>


### [203] [PhysMani: Physics-principled 3D World Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01938)
*Peng Yun,Shouwang Huang,Hao Li,Jinxi Li,Jianan Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysMani框架，结合物理原理的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知动作策略模型，通过在线优化学习无散度高斯速度场预测动态未来，并构建PhysMani-Bench基准，在仿真和真实实验中超越强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型在处理非结构化3D环境中的快速动态目标时，存在3D几何不准确和物理意义预测不足的问题。

Method: 1) 物理原理的3D高斯世界模型：通过在线优化学习无散度高斯速度场，实现快速且物理可信的未来动态预测；2) 未来感知动作策略模型：通过可学习token的交叉注意力模块集成预测的3D场景未来动态；3) 构建PhysMani-Bench动态操作基准（16个任务）。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，PhysMani在成功率和动态操作性能上均显著优于现有强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合物理约束的3D高斯世界模型能有效提升具身智能在动态非结构化环境中的操作能力，为快速运动目标的操控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Manipulating fast and dynamically moving targets in unstructured 3D environments remains challenging for embodied AI. Existing visual-language-action models and world models struggle with accurate 3D geometry and physically meaningful forecasting. We propose PhysMani, a framework that couples a physics-principled 3D Gaussian world model with a future-aware action policy model. The world model learns a divergence-free Gaussian velocity field via online optimization for fast and physically grounded future dynamics prediction. The policy model integrates the predicted 3D scene future dynamics through a learnable token based cross-attention module. We introduce PhysMani-Bench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark with 16 tasks, and demonstrate a superior success rate over strong baselines in both simulation and real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [204] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Way of Water系统，这是一个将自主水面船队作为分布式编舞平台的网络物理架构。核心贡献是Way of Water Studio——一个基于浏览器的时序合成创作范式，将船队视为类似DAW的乐器用于音乐响应式编舞，通过Sequential Convex Programming轨迹生成和Model Predictive Control扰动抑制实现，并在苏黎世湖（18艘船）和威尼斯双年展（8艘船）完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放水域的非线性流体动力学给机器人编舞带来巨大挑战，现有方法局限于2D水面像素范式，缺乏将3D体积域表达与高性能水下机器人控制相结合的、可供非程序员艺术家使用的创作工具。

Method: 构建垂直集成框架：浏览器端Way of Water Studio提供DAW式时间线合成范式；采用Sequential Convex Programming进行轨迹生成，Model Predictive Control实现扰动抑制；自研全向底盘、水环境状态估计与控制栈、LTE/MQTT船队链路及RTK-GPS时间同步。

Result: 在苏黎世湖18艘船《天鹅湖》演绎和威尼斯双年展8艘船演示中完成系统验证，证明了流体机器人集群的设计与部署可行性。

Conclusion: Way of Water为流体机器人集群的编舞设计提供了基础参考，将创作门槛降低至非程序员艺术家可用的水平，并将表达维度从2D水面扩展至3D体积域。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [205] [A Stereo Visual SLAM System Using Object-Level Motion Estimation and Geometric Filtering Based on Cross Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02005)
*Sujan Kumar Dhali,Bhaskar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCD SLAM是一种动态立体视觉SLAM框架，通过联合处理动态物体和动态特征，利用视差与交叉视差的不一致性检测动态特征点，并结合3D目标检测与卡尔曼滤波跟踪，在KITTI数据集上相比ORB-SLAM2实现了更高的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
